The question also keeps neighbouring failures distinct. Accurate reasoning
serving a destructive destination can remain destructive sublimation without
becoming un-reason. It becomes [**un-reason**](0030-epistemic-constitution.semantic-definition.md#un-reason-reasoning-that-defeats-the-conditions-of-reason)
when active reasoning disables the independent correction needed to challenge
that destination or its protected account. It approaches a [**Ragnarök
trajectory**](#program-reproduction-and-ragnarok-trajectory) only when successful
propagation increasingly strengthens a terminal organizing logic while consuming
the containing order or the programme's own conditions of continuation. Asking
the Grail question identifies the audit problem; evidence about beneficiaries,
objectives, sacrifices, correction, and temporal effects must answer it.

Civilization depends upon a **bundle of shared sublimations**:

```text
courtesy
+ honesty
+ temporal discipline
+ reciprocity
+ delayed gratification
+ evidential standards
+ lawful conflict resolution
+ responsibility
────────────────────────
civilizational character
```

Politeness formulas, turn-taking, punctuality, promise-keeping, due process, evidence, citation, protocol, and professional discipline can be understood as institutionalized sublimations. They store ways of transforming drives so that strangers can cooperate without a perfect system or constant supervision.

Formal institutions cannot substitute completely for character. Courts require people willing to tell unwelcome truths. Science requires investigators capable of relinquishing favoured claims. Contracts require some fidelity beyond immediate advantage. Democracy requires losers who remain members of the constitutional community.

[Thomas Sowell's *A Conflict of Visions*](https://www.hachettebookgroup.com/titles/thomas-sowell/a-conflict-of-visions/9780465002054/) captures the constrained-vision account with the deliberately sharp image that each new generation arrives as an invasion of civilization by “little barbarians” who must be civilized before it is too late. The point is not contempt for children. It is that no newborn inherits courtesy, self-command, truthfulness, punctuality, reciprocity, or work discipline in finished cultural form. Each generation must be initiated into the braid through largely unarticulated example, correction, practice, and expectation. Where formation does not take, a formally perfect system cannot compensate indefinitely for the missing character of its participants.

The originating formulation carries a harder consequence: people in whom this civilizing process does not take—because the formation was weak, because the surrounding mythology rewarded the opposite, or because the person resisted it—can become sources of antisocial conduct, of which crime is only one form. Mythology construction therefore concerns not decorative cultural refinement but the intergenerational production of adults capable of sustaining a cooperative civilization.

The formative sequence is:

```text
raw drive
   ↓
external boundary and example
   ↓
repeated practice
   ↓
habituation
   ↓
sublimation
   ↓
internalized character
   ↓
voluntary search for meaningful difficulty
```

#### Cultural sublimation architecture

The formative sequence describes a process. It should not be collapsed with the
durable cultural structure that makes the process recurrent or with the symbol
that calls the structure to mind:

| Analytical level | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Raw drive** | Energy or impulse available for direction |
| **Sublimation** | The transformation process through which that energy receives another form |
| **Sublimated form** | A resulting capacity, character disposition, relationship, practice, role, or institution |
| **Cultural sublimation architecture** | The durable mythology-bearing arrangement that selects, teaches, rewards, embodies, and transmits shared sublimations |
| **Symbolic carrier** | A phrase, story, song, hero, ritual, image, or exemplary act that activates relevant installed programming |

> A **cultural sublimation architecture** is the interlocking body of
> mythologies, limits, exemplars, roles, practices, systems, rewards, sanctions,
> institutions, and transmission pathways through which a culture recurrently
> transforms raw drives into character, capability, relationship, creation,
> maintenance, and posterity.

The architecture is **generatively central** when many downstream structures
depend upon the capacities it recurrently produces. Courts, science, contracts,
families, professions, markets, infrastructure, and constitutional government can
all depend upon different combinations of truthfulness, self-command,
reciprocity, delayed gratification, courage, care, and responsibility. Calling
the architecture *root-level* therefore makes a proposed claim about causal
dependency, not about metaphysical primacy, automatic moral goodness, or an
unchangeable historical form.

Generative centrality remains distinct from felt relevance. A mythological lens
may treat a particular family, institution, or outcome as morally important while
failing to represent the formative architecture upon which its continuity
depends. A contrary case is also possible: an inherited formation system may
receive great reverence while producing little legitimate capability or while
preserving coercion, falsehood, or subjecthood absorption.

```text
cultural sublimation architecture
          ↓ recurrent formation
character + capability + relationship + disciplined practice
          ↓ enable
families + professions + science + law + production + political order
          ↓ reproduce, neglect, correct, or consume
the architecture handed to the next generation
          ↺
```

One important failure pattern is therefore:

```text
high generative centrality
+ low felt relevance or active moral suspicion
→ weakened protection, practice, and transmission
→ continued consumption of inherited capabilities
→ delayed downstream institutional failure
```

This pattern is a hypothesis requiring independent measures of dependency,
formation, transmission, and consequence. It must not be inferred merely because
an inherited practice has declined.

#### The lifted sublimation-generator pattern

The originating proposal should be stated before its reconstruction:

> **The categorical imperative may be a generator of sublimations. Duty is its
> enduring bottom: the first binding capacity supplied by external culture, upon
> which richer character and capability can be built. From there, the self-author
> can take over.**

This proposal yields the **lifted sublimation-generator pattern**: a recurrent
functional organization in which an installed second-order rule-set generates
binding directions for changing situations and a formative architecture converts
those directions into durable character, capability, relationship, practice,
role, or institution.

The pattern should not be collapsed with the cultural sublimation architecture.
The **generator kernel** selects or produces a binding direction. The actuator
makes that direction motivationally effective. The cultural sublimation
architecture supplies the exemplars, practice, correction, rewards, roles, and
institutions through which the direction becomes a durable form:

```text
fixed meta-rules M
+ current knowledge K_t
+ situation C
→ binding direction R_t(C)

raw drive or motive E
+ binding direction R_t(C)
+ actuation A
+ formative practice and embodiment P
→ sublimated form S_t

consequences + criticism + encounter with reality
→ retain, correct, or rebraid M, K, R, A, or P
```

Here *fixed* retains its established lifted-solidarity meaning: operatively
installed and binding at the relevant time, not eternal or immune to correction.
The first line is the generator kernel. The second and third lines are necessary
to the complete sublimation-generator pattern. A philosophical maxim alone does
not train a capacity, supply motivation, build an institution, or close a
correction loop.

The neutral pattern and its normative assessment must remain distinct:

- A **lifted sublimation generator** can successfully transform drive into
  discipline and capability without being morally good.
- A **positive sublimation generator** is the evaluatively successful subtype
  proposed here: its outputs are life-supporting, person-preserving,
  capability-producing, reality-answerable, and corrigible.

The second expression does not turn structural efficacy into moral truth.
Disciplined persecution, compulsive sacrifice, obedient self-erasure, efficient
domination, and technically accomplished falsehood can all show formative success
without positive normative valence. *Positive* therefore states a contestable
evaluation against declared criteria and observed consequences.

A complete candidate specification should distinguish:

- the **selection kernel** that maps knowledge and situation to a proposed
  direction;
- any **hard constraints** that remove impermissible directions from
  consideration;
- the **value function** or evaluative destination by which remaining outputs and
  consequences are compared;
- the **actuator** that makes a selected direction motivationally binding;
- the **formative architecture** that turns repeated direction into capacity; and
- the **audit and feedback loop** that can correct inputs, outputs,
  interpretation, jurisdiction, or the generator itself.

*Kernel* and *value function* are controlled computing and optimization analogies,
not claims that moral development is deterministic numerical maximization. A
philosophical system may also combine or decline these roles rather than label
them separately. In the comparison below, Kantian universalizability functions
approximately as a selection test and Humanity-as-standing as a hard constraint;
Rand treats rational life and flourishing more nearly as the governing standard
of value.

One deliberately provisional example is the
[remove-the-me
test](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#impersonal-universality-against-private-interest):
a private reason is reformulated without “because it is me or mine” and tested
under exchanged positions. That operator illustrates lifted selection but does
not by itself guarantee truth, positive consequence, or preservation of persons.
The separate human-person constraint asks whether the output still treats every
concrete person as an end rather than a summand in an aggregate or an organ of a
collective subject.

##### Duty as first product and continuing infrastructure

Duty occupies two positions in the originating account:

1. **Duty as a first sublimation:** immediate impulse has become a capacity to
   bind action to a rule even when inclination changes.
2. **Duty as formative infrastructure:** the acquired capacity for self-command
   supports later honesty, courage, reciprocity, productive mastery, evidential
   discipline, promise-keeping, and lawful authority.

```text
external boundary + example + correction
→ internal capacity for rule-bound self-command
→ reflective use of that capacity
→ further character and capability
→ self-authored but still reality-bound development
```

“First” is architectural, not necessarily chronological. Attachment, imitation,
language, trust, turn-taking, and delayed gratification may precede recognizable
duty in development. Whether duty is chronologically the first moral sublimation
is an empirical question.

Nor is duty a set of training wheels discarded by the mature person. In the
Kantian configuration it remains the binding floor whenever inclination and moral
law diverge. The later self-author takes over **authorship responsibility**:
inspecting, applying, correcting, and where necessary rebraiding the inherited
generator. The self-author does not become cultureless, remove the local
bigOther, or empty the PCI slot. One successor weave remains fully installed
under single-slot exclusivity.

Kant supplies important source material without having formulated this
Culture Mechanics pattern. In the *Groundwork* he treats moral examples as
encouragements that show practicability without becoming the source of the law
([AA 4:408–409](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/409.html)). In the *Critique of
Practical Reason* he describes repeated moral judgment and smaller exercises as
forming a habit and gradually producing a pure moral interest
([AA 5:158–159](https://korpora.org/kant/aa05/159.html)). In the *Metaphysics of
Morals* he makes cultivation of intellectual, mental, and bodily powers a duty to
oneself while leaving the selection and proportion of capacities to cultivate to
the person's rational reflection about their powers and way of life
([AA 6:444–445](https://korpora.org/kant/aa06/445.html)).

##### Humanity as standing, horizon, and sacred object

The originating image and its principal danger should remain visible together:

> **Humanity is the abstract “human spirit”: the idea of what a human can be—
> something like *Star Trek*, “endlose Weiten,” and what human beings can
> positively achieve.**

> **Humanity can become either the sacred standing carried by every concrete
> person or an abstract collective idol to which concrete persons are
> sacrificed.**

The first formulation is an originating Culture Mechanics image, not Kant's
technical definition. Its useful reconstruction distinguishes three connected
aspects:

| Aspect | Meaning | Function in the generator pattern |
|---|---|---|
| **Humanity-as-standing** | The rational, end-setting, claim-bearing personhood that may never be treated merely as material | Hard constraint |
| **Humanity-as-horizon** | The open and inexhaustible space of what human beings might cultivate, create, understand, and become | Aspirational destination |
| **Humanity-as-sacred object** | The culturally represented abstraction through which attacks upon concrete persons become experienced as profanation | Affective protection and transmission |

**Humanity-as-standing** is closest to Kant's Formula of Humanity. Kant writes
that rational nature exists as an end in itself and commands that Humanity in
one's own person and every other person be treated always also as an end and never
merely as a means
([AA 4:428–429](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/429.html)). This concerns rational,
end-setting agency instantiated in persons. It does not make the species, state,
civilization, or a collective super-person called Humanity the supreme end.

**Humanity-as-horizon** asks a further Culture Mechanics question:

> **What might beings possessing Humanity-as-standing make of themselves?**

The horizon includes reason and understanding, moral character, craft, productive
mastery, imagination, art, exploration, courage, protection, fidelity, care,
plural cooperation, responsible institutional power, self-authorship, and
reality correction. No individual exhausts it. Human possibility is plural rather
than one standardized life or a single rank ordering.

**Humanity-as-sacred object** interprets Durkheim's religion of the human person.
Durkheim says that the *personne humaine* is considered sacred and that attacks
upon a person's life, liberty, or honour evoke horror analogous to profanation
([“Individualism and the Intellectuals,” pp.
46–47](https://platypus1917.org/wp-content/uploads/The-Heritage-of-Sociology-Emile-Durkheim-On-Morality-and-Society_-Selected-Writings-University-of-Chicago-Press-1973.pdf)).
In Culture Mechanics, Humanity-as-standing supplies a rule of equal
non-instrumental standing, while Humanity-as-sacred-object can give that rule
affective force in homo duplex.

The originating language of inheriting “a piece” of Humanity should therefore be
refined rather than erased:

> **Every person is a complete bearer of Humanity as moral standing while
> realizing only a partial and individual selection from the horizon of human
> possibilities.**

```text
moral standing: complete and equal in every person

developed capabilities: partial, plural, and radically unequal
```

Newton's achievement may be exceptional while Newton's standing as a person is
not greater than that of an unknown or unsuccessful person. Kant's distinction
between price and dignity supplies the relevant boundary: skill and diligence
have conditional value, whereas morality and Humanity insofar as it is capable of
morality possess dignity
([AA 4:434–435](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/435.html)).

This Humanity mythology consequently carries two complementary messages:

> **You already possess unconditional standing.**

> **You have not exhausted what you might become.**

The first prevents achievement worship and human expendability. The second
prevents dignity from becoming a merely passive status detached from cultivation,
responsibility, and contribution.

The complete Humanity architecture can now be stated without collapsing its
levels:

```text
Humanity-as-standing
→ hard constraint against instrumentalizing persons

Humanity-as-horizon
→ open destination space for cultivated capability

categorical imperative or rival generator
→ selects and regulates transformations

duty, pride, vocation, love, or honour
→ supplies binding and motivational force

kingdom of ends
→ relational order among different self-authoring persons

Leuchttürme
→ make particular human possibilities visible

Star Trek-like civilizational images
→ make a possible constellation of developed Humanity visible

reflective auditor
→ tests whether the generator still serves concrete persons and reality
```

Kant's ***Reich der Zwecke***—usually translated **kingdom of ends**—is not a
homogeneous collective person. It is his ideal of a systematic union of rational
beings through common laws in which each rational being belongs as both a maker
of universal law and one subject to it, and in which persons are ends in
themselves while their particular ends can coexist within the common order
([AA 4:433–434](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/434.html)). In the present
reconstruction it supplies the relational level missing from a merely private
ethics: distinct self-authoring persons do not dissolve into one will, but bind
themselves to a common generator that forbids treating one another merely as
instruments. This mapping must not obscure Kant's stronger claim that practical
reason itself legislates the law, or turn the kingdom into an empirical political
programme guaranteed by mythology construction.

The *Star Trek* image operates at a different scale from a Newton or an Einstein.
It is a **civilizational Leuchtturm**: an imagined constellation in which
curiosity, aggression, difference, intelligence, and material power have been
partly sublimated into exploration, protection, plural cooperation, and
responsibility. It is not a completed blueprint, proof that the depicted
institutions work, or one mandatory future. Individual and civilizational
*Leuchttürme* show what beings who already possess standing might become; neither
determines who counts as an end.

The central pathology is **Humanity inversion**:

```text
Humanity-as-horizon
→ Humanity agentified as a collective subject
→ Humanity receives an alleged “true interest”
→ privileged interpreters claim access to that interest
→ concrete persons become material for Humanity's development
→ sacrifice is renamed progress or emancipation
```

> **Humanity inversion occurs when an abstraction constructed to protect concrete
> persons becomes the authority that permits them to be consumed.**

Humanity inversion can produce
[subjecthood absorption](0010-culture-mechanics.semantic-definition.md#subjecthood-absorption-fusion)
and [*Herrschaftsideologie*](#herrschaftsideologie-privileged-vision-converted-into-jurisdiction).
“Humanity demands” and “the future of mankind permits no dissent” are
authorization bids unless jurisdiction and representation are independently
constituted and corrigible. Humanity has no self-appointed spokesperson merely
because someone invokes its name.

Several boundary questions remain open. Kant does not use *Menschheit* in one
unchanging technical sense across every work; in *Religion within the Boundaries
of Mere Reason*, for example, he distinguishes animality, humanity, and
personality ([AA 6:26–27](https://korpora.org/kant/aa06/026.html)). Accounts based
upon presently exercisable rational capacity also face difficult questions about
infants, profound cognitive disability, dementia, nonhuman animals, and possible
artificial rational agents. This semantic definition does not solve those cases
by stipulation.

##### Pattern-principal displacement: when differences become the higher good {#pattern-principal-displacement}

> **The pattern of differences can become the higher good.**

The assistant first proposed this sentence in the Humanism–Diversity comparison;
the project author explicitly selected it as an important insight. The
**pattern-principal displacement** name and the custody pathway below are
project-developed formalizations.

This originating formulation identifies a possible reversal in service
direction. Plural persons, cultures, practices, organisms, or habitats can be
valuable to one another or to a life-supporting whole. But when a selected pattern
of differences becomes the protected principal, its concrete bearers can be
reclassified as instruments for producing, preserving, displaying, or correcting
that pattern.

**Pattern-principal displacement** is the project term for this shift:

```text
plurality serves concrete persons and viable forms of life
→ selected differences receive independent sacred standing
→ a preferred composition or continuing recognition process becomes principal
→ persons, groups, cultures, or organisms become carriers, representatives,
   inputs, burdens, or obstacles relative to the pattern
```

The shift is possible across identity, cultural, institutional, and ecological
uses of *Diversity*, but it is not entailed by the word. Descriptive variety,
anti-discrimination, freedom of association, epistemic plurality, cultural
expression, ecological resilience, and protection against catastrophic
concentration can all remain bounded means serving independently specified goods.
The [nested, supplementary, and replacement
relations](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#humanism-diversity-relations)
identify the actual hierarchy. [**Strong Programmatic
Diversity**](0060-culture-program-ecology.semantic-definition.md#strong-programmatic-diversity)
names a robust Culture Program profile; it becomes a replacement relation only
when the pattern can override the standing of its bearers.

Difference cannot justify itself merely by being difference. More diseases,
coercive practices, crop pests, fraud techniques, or forms of corruption are not
made good by increasing variety. A Diversity claim therefore requires an
independently defended selector—such as liberty, knowledge, beauty, resilience,
human development, ecological continuity, rectification of demonstrated injury,
or protection against domination—and a rule for conflicts among those goods.

###### Metric custody in more detail {#metric-custody-in-more-detail}

A metric is not simply a measuring instrument that discovers a number already
waiting in reality. It is a constructed mapping:

```text
selected reality
→ selected objects and categories
→ selected observations
→ selected scale and baseline
→ selected weights and aggregation
→ score
→ deficit judgment
```

Written schematically:

```text
score = Mθ(observations)
```

`θ` contains the choices that define the metric:

- what entities count;
- how they are partitioned;
- which features matter;
- how differences are measured;
- spatial and temporal scale;
- baseline or counterfactual;
- weights;
- normalization and aggregation;
- treatment of uncertainty and missing data; and
- target, ordering, review, revision, and completion rules.

**Metric custody** means authority over those choices and their revision. It does
not merely mean possessing the measuring equipment or collecting the raw data.
The custodian controls the model through which selected reality becomes
administratively legible and through which observation can become a deficit
judgment.

*Constructed* does not mean fictional or unconstrained. Reality can defeat a
measurement, observations can be more or less valid, and one mapping can be
better fitted to a declared purpose than another. Construction names the
choices required to turn selected observations into one administratively usable
representation.

Nor is Diversity one self-interpreting quantity. Every genuine operative
Diversity or biodiversity measure instantiates some `Mθ`, whether or not its
parameters are made public. But the word can also be invoked without any stable
mapping at all. The same distinction applies to safety, equity, wellbeing,
representation, resilience, or another compressed good.

Those selections can stand in three importantly different relations to public
language:

| Case | What exists | Primary custody problem |
|---|---|---|
| **Explicit metric** | A disclosed quantitative mapping or operational rule specifies the object, dimensions, evidence, comparison, threshold, and result | Who selects, weights, applies, revises, certifies, and can challenge the rule? |
| **Tacit operational criterion** | A sufficiently stable qualitative or quantitative rule exists in professional convention, internal guidance, precedent, or recurrent practice, but is not publicly inspectable | Can affected persons reconstruct the rule, test consistent application, introduce counterevidence, and appeal its use? |
| **Pseudo-metric predication** | Comparative, threshold, or evaluative language gives the impression that an assessment occurred although no stable operational rule may exist | Who may convert an unexposed judgment into an apparently reality-supplied verdict and then act upon it? |

*Metric* does not mean *real numbers only*. A qualitative criterion can possess
operational reality when its purpose, relevant factors, comparison class,
boundary cases, evidential basis, treatment of uncertainty, and conditions of
defeat are sufficiently articulable for independent scrutiny and reasonably
consistent application. Conversely, adding a number does not make an arbitrary
ontology, baseline, weight, or threshold objective. The distinction concerns the
existence and corrigibility of the decision rule, not numerical appearance.

The third case is therefore not strictly a hidden metric:

> **There may be no hidden ruler. The language merely imitates the authority of
> having used a ruler.**

For example, *significant biodiversity value* leaves open the relevant dimension,
comparison, scale, threshold, purpose, and uncertainty while suggesting that a
threshold has already been crossed. *Adequate ecological integrity* is
necessarily adequate for some purpose, relative to some baseline, over some
period, but can function as an approval operator without disclosing any of them.
*Unacceptable biodiversity loss* is not a physical property of the loss; it is a
normative and institutional verdict that can be grammatically attached to Nature
as though Nature itself had issued it.

The ecological sequence is developed in the [Hanekamp--Wegner bridge on change
and deterioration](#change-deterioration-moral-appraisal). A metric can report a
change and a model can predict one; the transition to *deterioration* requires
the relevant-structure and moral-appraisal layer, while *unacceptable* adds a
further severity and authorization threshold.

The [linguistic inventory calls this compound operation **pseudo-metric
predication**](0030-linguistic-tricks.semantic-inventory.md#lt-043-pseudo-metric-predication).
Its generic sequence is:

```text
underspecified object
+ measurement-like or threshold predicate
+ authorized interpreter
→ apparent factual assessment
→ concealed or unexposed evaluative verdict
→ presupposed deficit
→ permission to intervene
```

The evaluation need not be false, insincere, or illegitimate. The specific
failure occurs when language presents a contestable judgment as though an
inspectable measure or operational classification had already supplied it. If a
stable internal rule is later found, the case becomes tacit criterion custody. If
no stable rule exists and each application is decided case by case, the primary
mechanism remains master-signifier and interpreter custody plus authorization
without specification. It can be more *Willkür*-enabling than a contestable
metric precisely because there is no fixed ruler against which inconsistency can
be demonstrated.

The decompression test asks:

> **Can the apparently measured predicate be stated as an object, dimension,
> baseline, scale, threshold or decision rule, evidence rule, uncertainty
> treatment, conflict rule, and possible defeater or completion condition?**

Failure to answer does not by itself prove manipulation; a term may be shorthand,
an early warning, an open-textured legal standard, or an honest expression of
uncertainty. The diagnosis strengthens when the phrase itself is treated as a
sufficient answer, authorizes binding action, displaces affected-person
testimony, is applied asymmetrically, or remains protected from decompression,
counterexample, review, and appeal.

> **Metric custody becomes policy custody when authority to choose those
> elements also determines which condition counts as a deficit, which remedy
> follows, who bears its cost, whether success occurred, and when intervention
> may stop.**

The full custody pathway is:

```text
ontology custody
→ metric and baseline custody
→ deficit classification
→ causal and moral interpretation
→ remedy and burden allocation
→ success, exception, and completion judgment
→ policy custody
```

That pathway tracks how representation can become policy. The
[**model-to-mandate custody
chain**](#model-to-mandate-custody-chain) supplies the larger epistemic and
constitutional route in which observations feed a metric, model, conditional
forecast, harm translation, protected-principal valuation, severity threshold,
remedy comparison, mandate, and outcome audit. Metric custody can therefore be
absent while later custody remains, or be present and legitimate while a later
causal, normative, or jurisdictional crossing fails.

This is strongest when the invoked principal is
[silent](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#silent-principal-surrogate-mechanism):
the abstraction, ecosystem, future generation, or desired pattern cannot dismiss
the surrogate who defines its interest. If the same custodian also controls
measurement, implementation, exception, and appeal, a surrogate-sovereignty and
*Willkür* affordance appears. Human judgment remains unavoidable, however.
Discretion becomes *Willkür*-enabling only when prospective constraint,
reciprocal standards, independent review, affected-person standing, correction,
succession, or repair are deficient; the mechanism alone establishes neither
capture nor *Willkürherrschaft*.

The [sacred-principal allocation
audit](0030-epistemic-constitution.semantic-definition.md#sacred-principal-allocation-audit)
therefore remains separate from drive classification. Nonhuman stewardship can be
reality-anchored and life-supporting. Humanist language can conceal a
person-sacrificing Humanity inversion. Pattern-principal displacement becomes a
death-drive or [Ragnarök-trajectory](#program-reproduction-and-ragnarok-trajectory)
candidate only with additional evidence of renewable negation, loss of credible
completion or correction, increasing programme capability, accumulated damage to
the carrying substrate, and terminal service direction.

The discriminating service-direction questions are:

1. Which differences are valued, by which prior good and at which scale?
2. Does the desired pattern serve concrete bearers, or may bearers be sacrificed
   to realize it?
3. Who controls the ontology, metric, baseline, causal story, remedy, exception,
   completion, and appeal?
4. Can mixed, unclassified, category-refusing, nonrepresentative, or
   pattern-conflicting persons retain standing and defeat the surrogate's account?
5. Would plausible alternative specifications reverse the ranking or remedy, and
   are those sensitivity results public?
6. Can an achieved repair count as completion, or does every outcome reproduce a
   successor deficit?

##### Aspirational exemplars and *Leuchttürme*

The originating formulation is:

> **Leuchttürme show, within some field, what “man” can be—what an individual can
> perhaps become and should consider aspiring toward.**

An **aspirational exemplar** is a real or represented person, character,
achievement, role, or civilizational image that makes a valued human possibility
visible, imaginable, and motivationally available. ***Leuchtturm*** is the
originating name and canonical lighthouse analogy for that construct.

Aspirational exemplars can:

- demonstrate that a capability or way of life is possible;
- orient aspiration without prescribing an identical life;
- condense virtues, disciplines, practices, institutions, and purposes;
- give emotional presence and *Beseelung* to an abstract generator;
- calibrate a perceived ceiling of human capability;
- make difficulty intelligible as movement toward a possible excellence;
- transmit psychological taste through story, image, biography, education, and
  imitation.

```text
abstract human possibility
→ embodied or represented by an aspirational exemplar
→ “a human being can become this”
→ admiration + counterfactual model of a possible self
→ acceptance of meaningful difficulty
→ practice and sublimation
→ capability, contribution, or a new exemplar
↺
```

This project formulation has strong neighbouring prior art rather than standing
alone. Morgenroth, Ryan, and Peters's [Motivational Theory of Role
Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1037/gpr0000059) distinguishes role models as
behavioural models, representations of the possible, and sources of inspiration.
Oyserman, Bybee, and Terry found that possible selves were more effective when
linked to plausible strategies, social identity, and a productive interpretation
of difficulty ([2006](https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-3514.91.1.188)). These findings
support parts of the proposed mechanism; they do not validate the entire
Culture Mechanics architecture or show that every admired figure functions as a
*Leuchtturm*.

At least five types should be distinguished:

- **domain Leuchtturm:** makes exceptional possibility visible in science, music,
  care, craft, engineering, statesmanship, or another field;
- **character Leuchtturm:** embodies a configuration of virtues across
  situations;
- **constructed or fictional Leuchtturm:** deliberately dramatizes a possible
  person;
- **civilizational Leuchtturm:** presents an imagined constellation of developed
  human capacities and institutions;
- **near exemplar:** supplies a more attainable step between the recipient and a
  distant horizon.

The user-originating physics–computer-science comparison is an illustrative
hypothesis: physics may possess more widely shared *Leuchttürme* such as Newton,
Einstein, and Planck, whereas fragmented computer science may give practitioners
fewer mutually recognized measures of extraordinary achievement. Turing,
Shannon, von Neumann, Knuth, Dijkstra, and others are obvious countercases.
Disciplinary age, specialization, team production, commercial secrecy, rapid
turnover, canon formation, and prestige institutions are rival explanations. The
general research question is whether fields with shared credible exemplars
develop different standards of aspiration, humility, apprenticeship, and
self-evaluation.

Kant supplies a sharp source boundary. Examples encourage and make practicability
visible, but they cannot replace the rational standard by which the exemplar is
judged. Rand supplies an unusually deliberate constructed case: the
[heroes of *Atlas Shrugged*](https://aynrand.org/novels/atlas-shrugged/)
dramatize reason, independence, productive achievement, and opposition to
sacrificial morality. Rand therefore gives her rival generator narrative bodies
and emotionally salient lives rather than propositions alone.

The lighthouse analogy is intentionally limiting:

> **A lighthouse makes a direction visible. It does not command the ship, choose
> its destination, propel it, or acquire jurisdiction over its crew.**

An exemplar demonstrates a possibility; it does not prove its entire philosophy.
Excellence in one domain supplies no automatic moral, epistemic, or political
jurisdiction elsewhere. Human standing is not graded by proximity to the
exemplar. A distant exemplar without attainable steps can produce passive worship,
envy, despair, or humiliation rather than practice. Surface imitation can copy
style while omitting discipline. Sanitized biography can conceal luck,
dependence, institutions, cruelty, failure, and cost. Halo expansion, personality
cult, prestige substitution, standardized Humanity, and conversion of excellence
into [*Herrschaftsideologie*](#herrschaftsideologie-privileged-vision-converted-into-jurisdiction)
are characteristic failure modes.

##### Kant and Rand as rival generator configurations

Kant and Rand illustrate competing configurations of the same abstract pattern:

| Dimension | Kantian configuration | Randian configuration |
|---|---|---|
| Starting authority | Universal rational law | Requirements of a rational organism's life |
| Selection principle | Universalizability and Humanity as an end | Reality-based contribution to individual life and flourishing |
| Binding floor | Duty and respect remain sufficient against changing inclination | Rational commitment to long-range values overrides whim |
| Characteristic sublimations | Self-command, integrity, impartiality, reciprocal law | Independence, productiveness, integrity, purpose, pride |
| Mythological destination | *Reich der Zwecke*, a community of self-legislating ends | The flourishing, productive, non-sacrificial individual |
| Central danger | Formal duty sanctifies false descriptions, sacrifice, or an authorized collar | CPU sovereignty is asserted while bigOther and field effects remain unexamined |

Rand's accusation should not be weakened:

> **Kant separates morality from life, happiness, and personal value, making
> sacrifice appear purer than flourishing.**

This is Rand's polemical verdict, not an uncontested interpretation of Kant.
Kant does not say that goodness must be painful; he recognizes duties to oneself
and includes happiness in the highest good. But the disagreement is real:
happiness and benefit do not ground moral validity for Kant. Rand's
[“The Objectivist Ethics”](https://courses.aynrand.org/works/the-objectivist-ethics/)
makes the individual's life the ethical purpose and human life the standard of
value, while [“Causality Versus
Duty”](https://courses.aynrand.org/works/causality-versus-duty/) attacks
unexplained duty as hostile to values and long-range life.

Rand is therefore not against sublimation. Her heroes are disciplined,
purpose-directed, emotionally enduring, and productively capable. She rejects:

```text
personal value → renunciation → sacrifice as proof of virtue
```

rather than:

```text
impulse → disciplined character → higher capability
```

Kant asks whether desire and advantage are constrained by universal standing and
persons-as-ends. Rand asks what concrete life, capability, value, or happiness a
demanded sacrifice actually serves. Culture Mechanics adds whether inputs,
framings, interpreters, outputs, and consequences remain corrigible by reality.
These are reciprocal diagnostics, not a claim that Kant and Rand secretly share
one ethics.

##### The reflective audit gate

The complete
[press-secretary–interpreter–auditor theory](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#reflective-roles-of-reason-press-secretary-interpreter-and-auditor)
has its semantic home in *Epistemic Agency under Mechanical Solidarity*. Within
the sublimation-generator pattern it functions as a reflective audit gate:

```text
lifted generator
→ binding output
→ duty or rational commitment
→ practice and sublimation
→ character and capability
        ↓
audit of generator + framing + authority + consequence
        ↓
retain, correct, unbraid, or rebraid
```

A positive generator must therefore produce more than disciplined recipients:

> **It must help produce persons capable of inspecting the generator that
> produced their discipline.**

External culture first supplies the capacity for rule-bound self-command. An
autonomy-directed lifted culture then makes reflective examination a second-order
obligation:

> **Control yourself sufficiently to examine, criticize, and, when necessary,
> revise what we have taught you.**

The counterfeit says: “Think for yourself—and you will know that you have done so
when you reproduce our authorized conclusions.”

Kant intends reason to audit maxims, private exceptions, heteronomous guardians,
and instrumental treatment of persons. Culture Mechanics asks a further
provenance question that Kant does not ask of the moral law itself: could what
presents itself phenomenologically as pure practical reason be a culturally
installed bigOther verdict? Rand similarly intends conscious reason to identify
life-serving values and retrain emotion; Culture Mechanics does not infer actual
CPU sovereignty merely because a philosophy declares it.

The combined project diagnostic is:

> **Does the generator transform impulse into durable capability while preserving
> every person's standing, remaining answerable to reality, supporting actual
> human flourishing, and permitting correction of its own applications and
> interpreters?**

This formula belongs to Culture Mechanics. It must not be attributed to Kant,
Rand, Durkheim, Haidt, or role-model researchers.

The complete constructive loop is:

```text
Humanity-as-standing
→ constrains the treatment of every concrete person

Humanity-as-horizon
→ opens a plural destination space

lifted sublimation generator + actuator
→ selects and binds situation-sensitive directions

formative architecture + Leuchttürme
→ make those directions practicable, visible, and desirable

sublimated character and capability
→ produce relationships, works, and institutions

reflective audit + finality cut
→ retain, correct, unbraid, or rebraid the operative generator
```

A lifted culture on this account does more than permit choice:

> **It supplies formative machinery capable of producing persons with
> self-command, aspiration, reflective audit, and eventual responsibility for the
> generator itself.**

Its pathological mirror is equally important:

```text
sacred duty
→ bigOther verdict mistaken for pure reason
→ CPU becomes press secretary
→ Leuchtturm becomes authority idol
→ Humanity becomes agentified collective subject
→ disciplined sacrifice appears autonomous and morally elevated
```

##### Discriminating questions and possible falsifiers

The pattern becomes analytically useful only if its parts can fail separately:

- **Liftedness:** Does binding force reside in a generator capable of producing
  changing outputs from knowledge and situation, or in a fixed catalogue whose
  abstract vocabulary merely disguises first-order answers?
- **Actuation:** Does a binding direction remain practically effective when
  inclination, prestige, surveillance, or immediate advantage changes?
- **Formation:** Can the claimed generator be linked to practices and
  institutions that actually produce the proposed capacities?
- **Normative valence:** Do the capacities protect persons and support life,
  relationship, capability, creation, maintenance, and posterity across affected
  groups, or merely improve obedience and institutional performance?
- **Humanity:** Does equal standing survive weakness, dependency,
  unproductiveness, error, and opposition? May persons refuse the declared
  civilizational destination?
- **Exemplars:** Does exposure change aspiration, practice, persistence, or
  capability beyond prestige display and self-selection? Are attainable pathways
  visible?
- **Audit:** Can alternative framings, counterevidence, failed consequences, and
  challenges to jurisdiction revise the generator or its applications?
- **Self-authorship:** Can recipients disagree with authorized interpreters
  without thereby being classified as irrational, immoral, or less human?

If the alleged generator neither produces changing outputs nor explains
formation; if its disciplined outputs repeatedly damage concrete persons while
remaining immune to consequence; if exemplars produce only prestige imitation;
or if audit never changes a protected verdict, the relevant claim should be
narrowed or rejected.

#### Symbolic condensation and counter-mythological reprogramming

Viewed through Wegner's compression and decompression language, a culturally
recognized representation of a sublimation can stand in for the larger
“machinery” behind it. A short phrase, song, hero, ritual, or exemplary practice
can evoke a much larger complex of discipline, purpose, honour, roles,
institutions, and expected conduct. **Sublimation emblem** is retained as a
Wegner-facing explanatory shorthand for this condensed representative function,
not as a new canonical culture-mechanical object type and not as a claim that
Wegner himself formulated the present sublimation theory.

In Culture Mechanics language, the carrier is normally an ideological symbol,
activation device, master-signifier-like token, or compressed fragment of
mythology. It does not contain the sublimation process or architecture. It
activates mappings already installed in the mythological lens, and the local
bigOther applies those mappings to produce culturally learned sentiments and
motivation:

```text
symbolic carrier
        ↓ activates
installed mythologies and lens mappings
        ↓ executed by
local bigOther
        ↓
honour / shame / attraction / aversion /
belonging / obligation / permission
        ↓
practice and public conduct
```

A rival or cynical mythology can attack the reproduction of a shared sublimation
by reclassifying the practice through which it is embodied. The central
intrapersonal target is not a freestanding decoder or prestige system. It is the
programming of the mythological lens:

```text
incumbent mythology:
effort or restraint → contribution, capability, honour
                         ↓ rival causal and moral story
effort or restraint → exploitation, foolishness, conformity, futility
                         ↓
symbols and stories activate the rival classification
                         ↓
repetition + systems + rewards + sanctions + observed conduct
                         ↓
reinforcement or reprogramming of the mythological lens
                         ↓
local bigOther supplies changed sentiment and motivation
                         ↓
changed practice, Beseelung, and intergenerational transmission
```

##### Illustrative carrier contrast: shared work and pause

The originating project example contrasts two short German carriers:

> **“Jetzt wird wieder in die Hände gespuckt”**

and

> **“Mach mal wieder Pause.”**

For a suitably programmed recipient, the first can activate a larger mythology of
shared exertion, productive contribution, competence, renewed effort, and
collective purpose. The second can activate interruption, relief, release from
demand, or withdrawal from exertion. Their importance for this example lies not
in their literal propositions but in the larger sublimation-related complexes
they can call:

```text
“Jetzt wird wieder in die Hände gespuckt”
        ↓ possible activation
shared work → contribution, capability, honour, common purpose

“Mach mal wieder Pause”
        ↓ possible activation
interruption → relief, release, distance from exertion
```

The proposed attack hypothesis is that an actor seeking to weaken an inherited
culture could try to make the second kind of carrier displace, ridicule, or
reclassify the first, supporting that substitution with counter or cynical
mythologies. If repeated through admired exemplars, systems, rewards, sanctions,
and observed conduct, the intervention could help reprogram the lens through
which effort and its supporting structures are classified:

```text
incumbent carrier activates an inherited sublimation
                         ↓
counter-carrier and cynical mythology reverse its classification
                         ↓
earnest effort becomes naïve, manipulated, or embarrassing
                         ↓ repeated cultural reinforcement
possible reprogramming of the mythological lens
                         ↓
weaker Beseelung, practice, and transmission
```

This is a project-supplied illustration, not empirical evidence that either phrase
has one invariant meaning, that a pause is culturally destructive, or that any
historical use constituted a coordinated attack. Context, audience programming,
institutional support, repetition, actual consequences, and evidence of intention
must be assessed separately.

Prestigious exemplars, ridicule, rewards, sanctions, and visible consensus matter
because they are programming and reinforcement channels. The codec perspective
remains useful for showing how a small token recruits a much larger installed
complex, but it must not install an additional decoder beside the main CPU and
local bigOther. Ordinary cognition reconstructs the represented situation; the
local bigOther executes the installed lens that culturally classifies it.

Cynicism has a distinctive corrosive possibility. A direct rival can say that an
ideal is wrong and offer another. Cynicism can instead make sincere aspiration,
discipline, gratitude, or service appear naïve, manipulated, or embarrassing
without accepting the burden of a functional replacement. In that case it can
reduce *Beseelung* and transmission before a successor architecture exists. This
is one specific route into the broader failure mode of [polemical
corrosion](#apologetic-closure-and-polemical-corrosion).

Several boundaries are essential:

1. A single signal ordinarily activates installed programming. Durable change
   requires reinforcement or reprogramming; an incompatible governing
   classification requires sufficient unbraiding and rebraiding under the
   single-slot principle.
2. Rest, play, contemplation, celebration, and resistance to exploitation can
   themselves be constructive sublimations or necessary conditions for durable
   capability. Exertion is not good merely because it is difficult.
3. Inherited sublimations can organize disciplined persecution, compulsive work,
   falsehood, domination, or subjecthood absorption. Corrective mythology and
   constructive resublimation must not be classified as corrosion merely because
   they weaken an incumbent practice.
4. Observed de-animation does not establish a deliberate attacker. Purposive
   cultural-program conflict, commercial selection, institutional hypocrisy,
   endogenous drift, justified criticism, and unintended consequence are rival
   causal explanations.
5. High *Beseelung* establishes affective animation, not truth, legitimacy,
   reality coupling, person-preservation, or flourishing.

### 22. Mythology as the education of psychological taste

The contrast between a very sweet drink and acquired tastes such as olives or dry wine supplies a developmental analogy.

Psychological childhood prefers intense, immediate, uncomplicated sweetness:

- gratification without delay;
- moral importance without achievement;
- certainty without inquiry;
- recognition without production;
- benefits without reciprocal cost;
- redemption without transformation;
- an enemy who explains every frustration.

Psychological adulthood learns to value complexity, restraint, responsibility, mastery, truth, and creation. Here adulthood names a developmental achievement rather than chronological age.

A civilizational mythology must therefore perform two operations:

```text
meet the recipient where existing desire can recognize an entry
                            ↓
lead that desire toward a more mature and generative form
```

Mythology construction is not merely taste matching. It is also taste education.

A mythology that maximizes present resonance without considering formation resembles a market that sells children the sweetest available drink. It can win immediate adoption while reducing the capacity to appreciate demanding goods. A formative mythology begins with available motives but attaches honour, belonging, and status to disciplines that expand the person.

If unlimited sweetness is supplied for long enough, the problem deepens: ordinary reality begins to taste intolerably bitter. Work, compromise, waiting, correction, and reciprocity then feel not merely difficult but like illegitimate deprivations. Taste education therefore changes both what is desired and what kinds of frustration can be borne without retreating into a downhill story.

[Herbert Marcuse's *Eros and Civilization*](https://www.marcuse.org/herbert/pubs/55erosciv/eccont.htm) represents one important dispute over sublimation. Marcuse distinguishes repression necessary to association from historically contingent “surplus-repression” and imagines less repressive sublimation. The present framework asks whether practices classified as surplus-repression may contain tacit civilizational knowledge and whether their removal produces liberation, constructive resublimation, or psychologically downhill desublimation. Marcuse is an illustrative case, not the foundation of the uphill/downhill distinction.

#### Marcuse as a linguistic dark artist

The originating judgment of this framework is sharper. Read operationally, Marcuse's liberation tends toward **liberation from civilization**: liberation from arts, habits, work discipline, sexual restraint, courtesy, authority, and moral formation insofar as these are reclassified as repressive sublimations. Marcuse understands his project as liberation *of* civilization from historically unnecessary domination. The present framework's verdict is that the attempted separation of necessary repression from “surplus-repression” can dismantle the inherited bundle of sublimations on which mature freedom depends.

```text
inherited restraint and discipline
              ↓ Marcusean classification
surplus-repression serving domination
              ↓
liberation authorizes removal of the restraint
              ↓ possible result
immediate gratification and desublimation
              ↓
weaker character, capability, and institutional maintenance
```

Marcuse is also treated here as a master practitioner of linguistic dark arts. The reading rule is not that every word has a mechanically inverted dictionary meaning. It is that an attractive moral noun may retain its emotional halo while a qualifier reverses the operation performed in its name.

His essay [“Repressive Tolerance”](https://www.marcuse.org/herbert/publications/1960s/1965-repressive-tolerance-fulltext.html) supplies an unusually clear example. Ordinary tolerance permits opposed views to exist and compete. Marcuse reclassifies that general tolerance as repressive, then explicitly defines **liberating tolerance** asymmetrically: intolerance toward movements of the Right and tolerance toward movements of the Left. The vocabulary of tolerance remains; the operational payload includes withdrawal of tolerance from selected speech, assembly, opinion, and movements.

```text
ordinary moral meaning
tolerance → permit opposed views
               ↓ ideological qualification
general tolerance → “repressive tolerance”
               ↓ moral inversion
tolerance now reproduces oppression
               ↓ action permission
selective intolerance becomes justified
               ↓ semantic relabelling
selective intolerance → “liberating tolerance”
```

This yields the **qualified-concept alarm**:

> When an otherwise clear and morally attractive concept receives an ideological qualifier, stop reading fluently. Translate the qualified expression into concrete actors, actions, permissions, and prohibitions.

The audit asks:

1. What did the unqualified word ordinarily mean?
2. Which cases has the qualifier removed from that meaning?
3. Which contrary operations has it introduced?
4. Who decides when the qualifier applies?
5. Is the revised rule symmetrical between allies and opponents?
6. What may now be done that the ordinary concept would have forbidden?

The governing distinction is:

```text
SEMANTIC WRAPPER
tolerance, liberation, autonomy, humanity
                    ↓ inspect
OPERATIONAL PAYLOAD
who may speak, who must yield, who decides,
which restraint is dismantled, and who may be suppressed
```

This case matters to mythology construction because the qualifier rewrites PCP:

```text
PERCEPTION  existing tolerance is not genuine tolerance
CONTEXT     the public sphere is already an instrument of repression
PERMISSION  suppressing selected views becomes an act of liberation
```

The dark art does not merely lie about a fact. It captures a prestigious word, reverses its permissions, and makes the reversal feel like fidelity to the original virtue.

### 23. Replicative success versus flourishing

A mythology can be fit as a replicator and destructive for its carriers.

```text
high resonance
+ immediate psychological reward
+ strong group enforcement
+ self-sealing causal story
──────────────────────────
rapid cultural replication

rapid cultural replication
does not imply
truth, agency, capability, prosperity, or survival
```

Mythology construction therefore distinguishes at least three success criteria:

1. **Replicative success:** Does the mythology spread and persist?
2. **Carrier success:** Do participants become more capable of flourishing, relationship, creation, and reality contact?
3. **System success:** Does the society maintain cooperation, correction, material viability, and intergenerational continuity?

A death-drive mythology may optimize the first while consuming the second and third. A constructive mythology must become culturally viable without sacrificing the people and institutions that carry it.

The governing contrast is:

> **gratification that consumes the future**  
> versus  
> **disciplined transformation that creates a future**

## Social coupling, scale, and collective agency

### 24. Coupling strength and coupling radius

A mythology can resonate intensely and still fail to support a society. Population-scale construction must consider not only the strength of a bond but the distance across which it remains effective.

The physical analogy uses two parameters:

- **coupling amplitude:** how strong obligation, trust, identification, and emotional synchronization are at close range;
- **coupling radius:** how far those bonds extend across kinship, locality, class, religion, profession, and subculture.

```text
clan-like order
high local amplitude + short radius
thick trust within, steep decay beyond

civic order
lower peak amplitude + long radius
thinner but generalized trust among strangers
```

The analogy is functional, not a claim that social bonds obey a literal physical force law.

Strong short-range coupling can sustain extraordinary sacrifice and mutual aid. It can also make impartial law, public office, and cooperation with outsiders difficult. Wider coupling supports markets, cities, professions, universal religions, federations, and citizenship, but may feel emotionally thinner and require institutional reinforcement.

At short range, the distinction can be felt in an ordinary emergency. A person may be able to summon several relatives at midnight with no written agreement while feeling little duty toward an unknown neighbour. A civic order asks for something less intimate but more extensive: predictable restraint, assistance, truthful dealing, and respect for common rules among millions of people who will never become kin. The first bond is thick and concentrated; the second is thinner but travels farther.

Mythology participates in this topology. Clan-centred mythology emphasizes ancestry, blood, honour, concrete loyalty, and remembered reciprocal obligation. Wide-net mythology emphasizes shared law, office, contract, universal dignity, citizenship, profession, or stewardship of a common world.

A constructor must therefore ask:

- At what social radius must cooperation occur?
- Which inherited bonds will support it?
- Which bonds will experience it as betrayal?
- Can a wider identity incorporate local loyalty without abolishing it?
- Does the mythology create bridges or only intensify one cluster?

### 25. Marriage rules as gene–culture coupling mechanisms

Marriage rules—including rules permitting, preferring, or forbidding first-cousin and other close-kin marriage—are unusually powerful culture strategies because they alter three networks simultaneously.

#### Reproductive network

Marriage affects who reproduces with whom, influencing relatedness, gene flow, population structure, and long-term selection pressures.

#### Social network

Marriage creates alliances, obligations, inheritance paths, and recurring cooperation between families. Endogamy concentrates these connections; exogamy distributes them.

#### Mythological network

Marriage determines which households repeatedly exchange stories, rituals, authorities, customs, moral concepts, and identities.

The proposed **marriage-mediated coupling-range hypothesis** is:

```text
prohibition of close-kin marriage
              ↓
partners sought beyond the immediate clan
              ↓
gene flow and alliances cross clan boundaries
              ↓
exclusive kinship coupling weakens
              ↓
social and mythological coupling reaches farther
              ↓
non-kin institutions become more useful and intelligible
              ↓
abstract civic identity becomes more plausible
```

One out-marriage creates a single bridge between two family clusters. Repetition across households and generations turns isolated bridges into a mesh through which obligations, stories, practices, and genes travel. The point is not that affection must become weaker in every individual marriage. It is that exclusive social closure around one dense kin cluster becomes harder to maintain when reproduction and alliance repeatedly cross its boundary.

The reverse hypothesis is:

```text
preferred close-kin marriage
              ↓
reproductive and social relations remain concentrated
              ↓
dense genetically and socially interrelated clans
              ↓
strong local obligation and short-range trust
              ↓
mythologies circulate mainly within the clan
              ↓
first-order mechanical solidarity remains dominant
```

[Jonathan Schulz, Duman Bahrami-Rad, Jonathan Beauchamp, and Joseph Henrich](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.aau5141) connect Western Church exposure, weakened intensive kinship, and global psychological variation. Their work supplies important prior evidence for investigating this mechanism; it does not by itself settle every causal step, genetic consequence, or application proposed here.

The genetics link is essential to the hypothesis. Marriage rules do not merely teach wider cooperation. Across generations they organize reproductive population structure while the resulting alliances alter cultural learning and institutional selection. They are therefore a direct gene–culture coupling mechanism.

### 26. First-order and lifted mechanical solidarity

[Émile Durkheim](https://classiques.uqam.ca/classiques/Durkheim_emile/division_du_travail/division_travail.html) uses **mechanical solidarity** for cohesion through likeness and a strong common consciousness, and **organic solidarity** for cohesion through differentiated interdependence.

The proposed term **first-order mechanical solidarity** names solidarity in which
members share concrete rules and answers. **Lifted mechanical solidarity** names
solidarity in which they share the rules that generate, select, apply, and revise
concrete answers:

> **fixed meta-rules M + current knowledge or inputs K_t + situation C → binding
> first-order output R_t(C)**

*Fixed* means operatively installed and binding, not eternal. Generator and
outputs are parts of one operative weave. A large body of detailed rules followed
as self-compulsorily proper is strong evidence of predominantly first-order
solidarity, but not its definition. A lifted order can also generate many detailed
rules; its binding sameness lies in the generator. [*Epistemic Agency under
Mechanical Solidarity*](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#25-examples-cases-and-boundary-tests)
contains the concrete religious, anthropological, hygienic, legal, market,
scientific, and procedural cases.

Lifting often permits people to reach different first-order answers while
remaining members of one order. It may therefore support plural lives, division of
labour, impersonal authority, and bounded jurisdictions. Those are important
possible consequences of the generator architecture, not its definition. The
mechanical element has not disappeared: participants are alike in treating the
answer-generating rules as binding.

Under single-slot exclusivity, both forms can produce **full capture**: one
operative weave completely occupies the local bigOther. First-order full capture
governs through shared concrete answers. Lifted full capture governs through one
shared answer generator. That generator may have universal jurisdiction and may
produce plural first-order outputs and bounded authorities. Whether either capture
is open and corrigible or closed and pathological is a separate axis.

This solidarity must be mythologically constructed and ritually renewed. A constitution printed on paper cannot by itself make losing an election non-existential, public office non-proprietary, or an opponent legitimate.

#### Authority migration across levels of solidarity

The movement from first-order to lifted solidarity may also change **who may speak
for the bond**. This is an authority-scaling hypothesis, not part of the
order-level definition. First-order fields are predicted to concentrate more
apologetic and polemical authority in embodied persons, whereas lifted fields can
locate more of it in persistent offices and institutions represented by replaceable
human beings. The lifted order does not eliminate persons; it can change the source
and continuity of the authority they carry.

This migration does not move universal jurisdiction from a person into one
all-powerful institution. The lifted meta-order is universal over the allocation
and limitation of jurisdictions; courts, universities, parliaments, churches,
professions, and public services represent and administer bounded parts of it. No
one office thereby owns all interpretation, still less reality itself. A persistent
institution can act across generations without receiving epistemic sovereignty.

This proposed migration also changes actor selection. A first-order field is
predicted to select more through personal embodiment of its concrete answers; a
lifted field can select through demonstrated competence within its procedures and
offices. Both remain mixtures of disposition and collective recognition. Neither
personal nor institutional authority becomes systemically operative through private
declaration alone.

A pathological transition can combine the disadvantages of both forms: institutional power without impersonal procedure, or personal charisma concealed behind institutional language. The design question is therefore not simply whether persons or institutions act, but whether authority, selection, succession, and correction fit the scale and content of the solidarity being defended.

Lifting is consequently one form of successor mythology construction. It unbraids
the finality of shared concrete answers and rebraids one shared generator that can
produce, apply, and revise answers. The successor may govern universally and may
retain old material as inputs or current outputs. It is not an exception to
complete slot occupancy or to unbraid/rebraid replacement.

### 27. Democracy export as a resonance problem

Liberal democracy presupposes more than ballots and institutions. Its mythology includes:

- the autonomous citizen;
- equality before impersonal law;
- loyal opposition;
- peaceful transfer of power;
- freedom of conscience;
- the right of political losers to remain full members;
- public office as a temporary trust rather than clan property;
- abstract reciprocity among strangers.

A ballot box can be manufactured, shipped, and installed. **Loyal opposition cannot be shipped in the same crate.** The latter depends upon a formed expectation that losing office does not expel a person or group from the moral community, that public power is temporary, and that an opponent can remain legitimate while being wrong. Democracy export fails when the visible operation arrives without the state of character and shared mythology from which the operation makes sense.

The **democracy-export hypothesis** is:

> Democratic institutions will remain fragile when the lifted civic mythology that makes them intelligible fails to resonate with the receiving population's dispositional distribution, inherited culture, kinship topology, and authority structure.

Under mismatch, imported forms can be assimilated into older solidarities:

```text
election        → census of clans or sects
party           → patronage vehicle
public office   → property of the victorious group
opposition      → betrayal
electoral loss  → existential dispossession
law             → instrument of relational power
```

The visible operation has been transmitted, but the state from which the operation makes sense has not.

The stronger genetic version predicts that populations may differ partly in the distribution of polygenic dispositions supporting impersonal trust, individual autonomy, pluralism, non-kin cooperation, and tolerance of legitimate disagreement. This remains a direct research hypothesis, not a claim that a population is biologically incapable of democracy.

#### The genetic-hardware hypothesis

The blunt originating formulation should not be hidden:

> **Some genetic hardware may not be made for lifted mechanical solidarity.**

“Not made for” does not mean metaphysically incapable. It means that the historically formed distribution of dispositions in one population may make an abstract civic mythology run naturally, while another population must spend far more institutional and formative energy to maintain the same operation.

The relevant candidate hardware includes dispositions affecting:

- autonomy from kin and inherited authority;
- trust in strangers and impersonal procedure;
- willingness to separate office from family possession;
- tolerance of moral and religious pluralism;
- acceptance of abstract rather than face-to-face reciprocity;
- ability to lose power without treating loss as expulsion from the community;
- willingness to regard an opponent as wrong but legitimate.

The software analogy is exact enough to be useful. The democratic procedure is an operation; lifted solidarity is part of the state from which the operation makes sense; dispositional distributions are part of the hardware on which that cultural state must run.

```text
same imported democratic operation
                 ↓
hardware and mythology fit
→ loyal opposition, impersonal office, peaceful succession

hardware and mythology mismatch
→ clan census, patronage, winner-takes-all possession, existential defeat
```

The test is comparative and prospective. If measured dispositions or future polygenic scores predict which populations and individuals sustain lifted-solidarity practices under otherwise similar institutions, the hardware hypothesis gains support. If institutional history and current incentives exhaust the prediction, its genetic component loses support.

Institutional coadaptation may bridge the mismatch through federalism, communal autonomy, local self-government, power sharing, customary institutions, and gradual construction of an overarching civic mythology. Such arrangements coordinate existing solidarities while a wider one develops.

### 28. Coordination, synchronization, and the coupling thermostat

Collective agency does not require everyone to think, feel, or act identically.

> **Coordination and synchronization are the two ends of a single continuum of interaction strength.**

**Coordination** arranges differentiated contributions toward a compatible result.
**Synchronization** aligns timing, attention, feeling, interpretation, judgment,
or action. Here **interaction strength** primarily means pressure toward common
phase or response, not the total functional dependence among specialists. The
continuum is an ideal-typical reader model: real groups can combine differentiated
roles with temporary synchrony, and specialist measurement may require several
variables without replacing this governing picture.

Three **crowd modes** make the continuum vivid:

| Crowd mode | Organizing pattern | Characteristic strength | Example and boundary |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Random crowd** | Shared space without a common task; weak coupling for the variable under study | Heterogeneity and mere co-presence | Saturday shoppers in a plaza; they are not literally statistically independent in every respect |
| **Clockwork crowd** | Differentiated roles routed through handoffs, or independent estimates externally aggregated | Functional interdependence in the routed form; error-cancelling variety in the aggregative form | A pit crew is routed clockwork; a stock-prediction tournament is aggregative clockwork only when an external mechanism combines the entries |
| **Laser crowd** | Alignment around when and how to attend, feel, interpret, judge, or act | Speed, force, courage, shared attention, rhythm, and coherent action | A chanting stadium, military drill squad, ritual assembly, or flock-like turn; synchrony alone does not establish truth or collective agency |

The clockwork family therefore contains two mechanisms. **Routed clockwork** needs
interfaces, handoffs, and controlled communication among differentiated roles; it
can have high functional dependence and should not be described as thriving on
minimal cross-talk in every respect. **Aggregative clockwork** preserves
independence among contributors so that dispersed, approximately unbiased errors
can cancel when an external mechanism combines their outputs. The whole
aggregation system is clockwork-like, but the contributors alone need not be a
strongly coupled crowd.

The project-developed Durkheimian correspondence is:

```text
organic solidarity    ↔ clockwork coordination
mechanical solidarity ↔ laser synchronization
```

The relation is resemblance and characteristic affinity, not identity. Organic
solidarity is cohesion through differentiated interdependence; clockwork is an
organizational mode. Mechanical solidarity is cohesion through shared
classifications and sentiments; laser synchronization is a dynamic process. Both
solidarities can coexist. [*Epistemic Agency under Mechanical
Solidarity*](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md), “Crowd modes,
collective effervescence, and Durkheim's solidarities,” gives the fuller
sociological treatment.

Lifted mechanical solidarity can supply the common moral or constitutional
protocol under which differentiated clockwork roles remain legitimate and
mutually intelligible:

> **The laser moment periodically regenerates the moral protocol used by the clockwork system.**

Some tasks require both modes; others become less intelligent when synchrony
suppresses independent perception.

```text
too little common meaning
→ fragmentation and inability to act together

too much synchronization
→ conformity, common-mode error, and loss of correction

viable coupling
→ sufficient shared meaning for cooperation
  + sufficient independence for perception and learning
```

The **coupling thermostat** is the mythology-construction problem of calibrating this balance.

Useful synchrony can provide:

- courage under danger;
- rapid emergency action;
- ritual solidarity;
- shared attention;
- confidence that others will perform complementary duties.

Excessive synchrony can produce:

- emotional escalation;
- identity fusion and, when generalized across jurisdictions, subjecthood
  absorption;
- suppression of minority perception;
- synchronized institutional error;
- inability to change course without collective humiliation.

A society-wide mythology should normally synchronize high-level identity and constitutional procedure more strongly than detailed empirical judgment. It should enable people to act together without requiring them to see every fact through one authorized lens.

The coupling thermostat is a project-developed engineering metaphor and research
programme, not an established device or Santa Fe Institute framework. The Santa
Fe Institute's call for a [“crisis discipline” of global collective
behavior](https://santafe.edu/news-center/news/communication-technology-study-collective-behavior-must-be-crisis-discipline-researchers-argue)
emphasizes that technology changes information flow at global scale while its
functional consequences remain poorly understood. It does not document a system
that senses a universal synchrony parameter and uses AI to steer stadium-scale
crowds.

An operational thermostat would require at least:

1. **sensors** for task-relevant states such as synchrony, functional dependence,
   error, diversity, confidence, and common-source dependence;
2. a task-specific **target range**, because no synchrony level is optimal for
   every purpose;
3. an **actuator**, such as changing information visibility, timing, group
   composition, or access to an independent counter-idea;
4. a **controller** connecting the observed state to intervention;
5. **evaluation** of accuracy, stability, displacement effects, and unintended
   feedback;
6. **constitutional limits** determining who may select the target, operate the
   controller, inspect it, contest it, and stop it.

Real-time social-signal meters and platform interventions can supply components of
such a system. They do not establish a validated, task-general, or ethically
legitimate coupling thermostat.

That qualification does not mean the component research is merely hypothetical.
Several neighboring programmes now occupy different layers of the problem:

| Research layer | Representative work | Contribution to mythology construction |
|---|---|---|
| **Stewardship and adaptation** | [Bak-Coleman et al. (2021)](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2025764118), [Galesic et al. (2023)](https://doi.org/10.1098/rsif.2022.0736), and [Lee, Krakauer, and Flack (2024)](https://doi.org/10.1098/rspb.2024.1606) | Treat information architecture as consequential, relate social-learning strategies and network structures to changing problems, and model how an adaptive system should match its learning timescale to environmental change; the last result is a distant organism--environment analogy rather than a crowd-control result |
