estimates and the institutional reply disputing Hanekamp, Briggs, and Rotgers's
stronger no-skill inference. Van Dobben and colleagues supply the 2025 account of
how empirical critical-load ranges become unique official values. RIVM's 2026
multi-model comparison supplies further mixed model-performance and deposition-
validation evidence. These sources do not establish one timeless verdict about
every AERIUS or OPS version, application, habitat, permit, farm, or remedy.

Sowell supplies several related source layers. *Knowledge and Decisions* supplies
the knowledge, incentive, feedback, and decision-process direction;
*Intellectuals and Society* supplies the analysis of intellectual influence under
weak empirical consequence; the **“Teflon prophets”** image names reputations
that survive repeated predictive failure; and *Judicial Activism Reconsidered*
supplies his source-side criticism of judicial law-making beyond constituted
interpretation. The **model-to-mandate custody chain**, **scientistic
authorization**, **Teflon-authority insulation**, **prediction exposure**,
**prediction ledger**, **decision--correction custody**,
**surrogate-correction asymmetry**, the typed legal-surrogate branch, and the
complete Dutch authorization-chain decomposition are Culture Mechanics
developments. Sowell's criticism informs them but does not supply their complete
definitions, conjunctions, audits, or constitutional safeguards.

The project's legal branch also preserves an attribution ladder that should not
be retroactively assigned to Sowell: vague or open-textured language is an
affordance; effective surrogate rule-making is a further institutional operation;
and group-interest adjudication, capture, or motive requires still further
evidence. Nothing in the bridge establishes that every interpreter, judge,
agency, court, expert body, delegated authority, or open-textured standard is
arbitrary or illegitimate.

#### Compression, reconstruction, and cognitive relief

**Status: source observations operationalized across the theory family.** Wegner's
account of linguistic *Komprimierung* and recipient-side *Dekomprimierung* and his
situated formulation “Denken tut weh” are retained as source material. [*Epistemic
Agency under Mechanical
Solidarity*](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#audience-modelled-reconstruction-and-decompression)
is the primary semantic home for **audience-modelled reconstruction**, its
adversarial subtype, the decompression audit, the authorization payload, and the
cognitive-relief hypothesis. Their typed models, audit fields, mediation claims,
tests, and the *lossy codec* and *decoder-targeted compression* analogies are
Culture Mechanics developments. They should not be retroactively attributed to
Wegner.

Wegner's separate [official *Talking
Points* page](https://www.dushanwegner.com/buecher/talking-points/) supplies the
forceful maxim that political language seeks trust and power over people's lives
and explicitly presents *Talking Points* as complementary to *Relevante
Strukturen*. The maxim belongs to Wegner; **political language as an authorization
bid**, the typed authorization payload, and its constitutional audit are the
project's translation and operationalization.

#### *Herrschaftsideologie*

**Status: pre-existing German compound narrowed and mechanized here.**
*Herrschaftsideologie* has heterogeneous prior uses for ideologies that legitimate
or stabilize rule. The title of Ernst Topitsch's [*Die Sozialphilosophie Hegels
als Heilslehre und
Herrschaftsideologie*](https://books.google.com/books/about/Die_Sozialphilosophie_Hegels_als_Heilsle.html?id=H1IQAQAAIAAJ)
(first published 1967) establishes earlier use of the compound. Björn Wendt's
[discussion of sustainability as a possible
*Herrschaftsideologie*](https://publikationen.soziologie.de/index.php/kongressband_2018/article/view/1001)
uses the term in the broader sense of an ideology that reflects, legitimates, and
stabilizes an existing order. A neighbouring Weberian premise is that claims to
power or authority require
[self-justification](https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1007/978-3-658-44422-8_2).

The project's specific definition--self-legitimation first, privileged vision,
agentified higher subject, conversion of epistemic asymmetry into jurisdiction,
and a closed interpretive loop--is not attributed to Topitsch, Wendt, or Weber.
The originating formulation and mechanism belong to the present synthesis.

#### bigOther

**Status: borrowed and substantially extended.** The spelling **bigOther** adapts [Jacques Lacan's big Other](https://wwnorton.com/books/9780393329254) into the culturally installed sentiment-classifier of culture mechanics. This is not Lacan's own computational or systems model.

#### Constitutive lack, desire, fantasy, demand, jouissance, death-drive, and life-drive

**Status: Lacanian concepts, selectively adopted and culturally operationalized
here.** The operative meanings of death-drive and life-drive come from [Jacques
Lacan's *Seminar
XI*](https://www.seuil.com/ouvrage/le-seminaire-livre-xi-jacques-lacan/9782020027618)
and [“Position of the Unconscious” in
*Écrits*](https://wwnorton.co.uk/books/9780393329254-ecrits), together with his
concepts of lack, desire, fantasy, demand, jouissance, repetition, and the drive
circuit. [Slavoj Žižek's *How to Read
Lacan*](https://www.lacan.com/essays/howread.html) supplies the present
formulation of fantasy as teaching desire and positioning the subject in relation
to what others want from them. Freud is the historical precursor to Lacan's
discussion, but the terms are not used here in a merely Freudian biological or
dual-instinct sense.

**Constitutive lack as persistent motivational gradient** is adopted only as an
optional Culture Mechanics motivational hypothesis. It is not processor duality,
single-slot occupancy, or a required premise of the collective-field account.
**Fantasy as a desire-organizing and subject-positioning schema** remains distinct
from mythology as a culturally communicable world model and operative programme.
**Finite operational demand** and the three-way comparison among open desire,
finite operational demand, and renewable demand are project-developed analytical
categories; they do not replace Lacan's need--demand--desire triad. Jouissance is
kept distinct from *Beseelung*: one names paradoxical drive-satisfaction, the other
the degree of affective animation of a cultural programme.

Lacan treats life-drive and death-drive as two aspects of drive and describes
every drive as virtually a death drive. The present synthesis extends that
structure into a cultural diagnostic: **death-drive mythology** names
self-reproducing repetition and jouissance that consume their human or
institutional carriers, while **life-drive mythology** names symbolic binding and
sublimation that accumulate capability, relationship, creation, maintenance, and
posterity. Those society-level criteria are the framework's application, not
quotations from Lacan.

#### Sublimation

**Status: borrowed and extended.** The term derives principally from [Sigmund Freud's psychoanalysis](https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/78221). The present framework extends it from individual drive transformation into a theory of shared institutional practices, moral formation, and civilizational character.

**Cultural sublimation architecture**, **generative centrality**, and the named
activation and reprogramming hypotheses are project developments. The
Wegner-facing shorthand **sublimation emblem** joins the project's sublimation
theory to Wegner's compression and decompression language; it is not presented as
Wegner's own theory of sublimation and does not replace the canonical
Culture Mechanics classification of the token as a carrier, ideological symbol,
master-signifier-like signal, or mythology fragment according to function.

#### Kant, Rand, Humanity, and aspirational exemplars

**Status: external source material reconstructed through a project-developed
pattern.** Kant supplies universal law, Humanity as an end in itself, duty,
self-cultivation, public reason, and the kingdom of ends; he does not formulate
the local bigOther, the lifted sublimation-generator pattern, or the
press-secretary audit. Primary source anchors include
[AA 4:408–409](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/409.html),
[AA 4:428–429](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/429.html),
[AA 4:434–435](https://korpora.org/kant/aa04/435.html),
[AA 5:158–159](https://korpora.org/kant/aa05/159.html), and
[AA 6:444–445](https://korpora.org/kant/aa06/445.html).

Ayn Rand's
[“The Objectivist Ethics”](https://courses.aynrand.org/works/the-objectivist-ethics/)
and [“Causality Versus
Duty”](https://courses.aynrand.org/works/causality-versus-duty/) supply the rival
life-standard configuration and the polemical accusation that duty ethics severs
morality from life, value, and happiness. **Kantian and Randian generator
configurations** are this project's comparison, not a shared category proposed by
either philosopher.

Durkheim's sacred human person supplies a source neighbour for
**Humanity-as-sacred-object**. **Humanity-as-standing** adapts Kant's Humanity
formula; **Humanity-as-horizon**, the two-message construction, and **Humanity
inversion** are project developments. The horizon must not be attributed to Kant
as his technical definition of *Menschheit*.

The *Leuchtturm* formulation is originating Culture Mechanics language.
Morgenroth, Ryan, and Peters's [Motivational Theory of Role
Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1037/gpr0000059) and Oyserman, Bybee, and Terry's
[possible-selves research](https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-3514.91.1.188) supply
neighbouring evidence for behavioural modelling, possible selves, inspiration,
attainable strategies, and interpretations of difficulty. They do not establish
the complete Culture Mechanics construct.

#### Topos and mythos

**Status: borrowed and extended.** *Topos* and *mythos* come from [Aristotle's rhetoric](https://plato.stanford.edu/entries/aristotle-rhetoric/#Top) and [poetics](https://classics.mit.edu/Aristotle/poetics.html). Here *topos* includes recurring cultural scenes as well as argument locations, while *mythos* includes society-wide causal-historical plots as well as dramatic plot structure.

#### Master signifier

**Status: borrowed and operationally extended.** The term comes from [Jacques Lacan's *Seminar XVII*](https://www.seuil.com/ouvrage/le-seminaire-livre-xvii-jacques-lacan/9782020130448) and enters political discourse theory through Laclau. Here it also names any compact symbol capable of activating a mythology's wider classification field; that design-oriented use is not identical to Lacan's S1.

#### Sacred values and sacred objects

**Status: borrowed and extended.** Protected and sacred values are developed by [Jonathan Baron and Mark Spranca](https://doi.org/10.1006/obhd.1997.2690), [Philip Tetlock and colleagues](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/10821194/), and [Scott Atran, Jeremy Ginges, and collaborators](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1863499/). The present definition broadens the object from trade-off-resistant values to symbols, places, practices, relationships, and institutions treated as morally non-fungible.

#### Moral taste buds

**Status: borrowed metaphor, expanded machinery.** [Jonathan Haidt and collaborators](https://moralfoundations.org/publications/) use the taste-receptor analogy for plural, partly evolved moral foundations whose expression is culturally elaborated. The present framework retains that idea but expands the species-typical “machinery” beyond the enumerated foundations to narrative learning, motivation, social imitation, and collective coordination. The expanded list is not Moral Foundations Theory itself.

#### Active gene–environment correlation and gene–culture coevolution

**Status: established mechanisms given a new target hypothesis.** Active
gene–environment correlation comes from [Robert Plomin, John DeFries, and John
Loehlin](https://doi.org/10.1037/0033-2909.84.2.309) and [Sandra Scarr and Kathleen
McCartney](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/6683622/); gene–culture coevolution was
pioneered quantitatively by [L. Luca Cavalli-Sforza and Marcus
Feldman](https://www.santafe.edu/people/profile/marc-feldman) and developed by
Boyd, Richerson, Henrich, and others. Haidt did not originate the mechanism; his
chapter 9 synthesis supplies the project's immediate fast-evolution bridge between
gene–culture coevolution and historically recent change. The present framework's
claim that these mechanisms may amplify and select differential **mythology
receptivity** is a new, testable extension rather than an established result of
those source theories.

#### Interpassivity

**Status: borrowed and extended.** [Robert Pfaller](https://edinburghuniversitypress.com/book-interpassivity.html), building on a line also associated with [Slavoj Žižek](https://www.versobooks.com/en-gb/products/1600-the-plague-of-fantasies), uses interpassivity for delegated consumption, belief, or enjoyment. The present framework extends it to delegated moral and political performance. That extension is a hypothesis, not Pfaller's established definition.

#### Paramorality

**Status: borrowed, narrowed, and detached from its source theory.** The term is adapted from [Andrew M. Łobaczewski's *Political Ponerology*](https://books.google.com/books/about/Political_Ponerology_A_Science_on_the_Na.html?id=H1GYNFhCYvYC). The present definition does not adopt Łobaczewski's broader ponerological theory; it uses *paramorality* only for the observable reclassification of destructive gratification as moral conduct.

#### False consciousness

**Status: borrowed and repurposed.** [Friedrich Engels used “false consciousness” in his 1893 letter to Franz Mehring](https://www.marxists.org/archive/marx/works/1893/letters/93_07_14.htm) within a historical-materialist account of ideology. Here it is examined as a mythology-construction operation that can redescribe ordinary acceptance as evidence of capture. The present use is therefore not identical to Engels's theory.

#### Repressive and liberating tolerance

**Status: Marcuse's terms, adversarial interpretation added here.** The expressions are Herbert Marcuse's. His [“Repressive Tolerance”](https://www.marcuse.org/herbert/publications/1960s/1965-repressive-tolerance-fulltext.html) supplies the object analysed here. **Qualified-concept alarm**, **semantic wrapper**, and **operational payload** name the present framework's adversarial reading method rather than Marcuse's own categories.

#### Suicidal empathy and the wood-cricket analogy

**Status: borrowed example, theoretically extended.** **Suicidal empathy** and its application to “Queers for Palestine” are attributed to [Gad Saad](https://the-atlas-society-presents.castos.com/episodes/when-empathy-becomes-suicidal-with-gad-saad). The present synthesis places the term inside egalitarian-guardian psychology, PCP, downhill mythology, and the distinction between carrier success and mythological replication. Saad's political interpretation should not be confused with the [experimental biology of hairworm-infected crickets](https://doi.org/10.1046/j.1420-9101.2002.00410.x).

#### Mechanical solidarity

**Status: source term retained; modifiers newly proposed.** [Émile Durkheim's mechanical solidarity](https://classiques.uqam.ca/classiques/Durkheim_emile/division_du_travail/division_travail.html) is retained, while **first-order mechanical solidarity** and **lifted mechanical solidarity** are proposed extensions distinguishing shared concrete rules and answers from shared rules that generate, select, apply, and revise answers. Those modified expressions are not Durkheim's. Plural civic integration is one proposed application of lifting rather than its definition.

#### Resonance

**Status: common metaphor given a new technical definition.** The word is common across physics, sociology, cultural theory, and communication. Here it receives a precise three-component meaning: fit among species-typical machinery, heritable dispositional configuration, and mythological form.

#### Construction

**Status: ordinary and cross-disciplinary term specified here.** The term shares family resemblance with social construction, discourse formation, framing, hegemony, cultural evolution, and institutional design. It does not imply that the object is unreal or that one designer controls the result.
The two-path distinction between **endogenous mythology formation** and the open
research possibility of **purposive mythology construction**, together with the
\(D^*\) trajectory-intervention model, belongs to the present synthesis.

#### Apologetics and polemics

**Status: theological terms generalized and functionally reassigned.** [John G. Stackhouse](https://academic.oup.com/book/12859/chapter/163145885) describes defensive apologetics as responding to challenges and offensive apologetics as provoking interest. [Friedrich Schleiermacher's *Brief Outline of the Study of Theology*](https://openlibrary.org/books/OL7182960M/Brief_outline_of_the_study_of_theology) is an important source for treating apologetics and polemics as distinguishable sides of philosophical theology. The present framework borrows the ordinary defensive–contestatory contrast but changes the object: **mythological apologetics** defends and repairs any installed mythology, while **mythological polemics** attacks and unbraids a rival mythology. These operational meanings are not identical to Stackhouse's or Schleiermacher's theological classifications.

#### Psychoanalysis as unbraiding and propaganda as rebraiding

**Status: source practices joined by a new interpretive analogy.** [Sigmund Freud's psychoanalysis](https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/38219) and [Edward Bernays's account of propaganda](https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Propaganda) are historical practices with their own objects and methods. The description of Freud as structured **untying** and Bernays as structured **weaving**, and the presentation of the two relatives as complementary programmers of the co-processor, belong to the present synthesis. They should not be attributed to either man as his self-description or treated as proof that Bernays merely applied Freud.

#### Critical Theory and cynical corrosion

**Status: source tradition retained; adversarial source formulation and
project-developed diagnosis kept separate.** [Max Horkheimer's “Traditional and
Critical Theory”](https://doi.org/10.5840/zfs19376265) gives Critical Theory a
positive rational and empirical orientation. James Lindsay's [“The Essence of
Critical
Theory”](https://newdiscourses.com/2025/11/the-essence-of-critical-theory/)
supplies the quoted adversarial formulation of delegitimation through comparison
with an unrealizable ideal. [Helen Pluckrose and James A. Lindsay's *Cynical
Theories*](https://www.ipgbook.com/cynical-theories-products-9781634312028.php)
supplies related critical prior art concerning postmodern and activist theories.
The framework treats the proposed operation as a **polemical failure mode**, not
Horkheimer's definition, a synonym for all Critical Theory, or proof of Lindsay's
“almost always” frequency claim. **Cynicism as corrosive acid**, **polemical
corrosion**, and their position in the one-slot replacement model are formulations
of the present synthesis rather than quotations from Pluckrose or Lindsay.

#### Critique–construction asymmetry

**Status: project-developed historical-mechanistic hypothesis.** The hypothesis
draws an adversarial source lead from Kenneth Minogue's *Alien Powers* and
reconstructs it through the framework's braid–unbraid–rebraid,
finite-versus-renewable-demand, and institutional-selection machinery.
**Critique–construction asymmetry** and **negative-operational displacement** are
not Minogue's terms, not definitions of Marxism or Critical Theory, and not claims
that the relevant traditions had no positive ends. The historical lineage and the
cross-programme generality require separate evidence. Minogue's “suicide pact”
and “attempt to destroy humanity” conclusions remain attributed terminal
judgments reached through distinct arguments; neither is the definition of the
hypothesis, an intent attribution, or by itself a Ragnarök-trajectory diagnosis.

### 42. Newly proposed terms and formulations

The following terms should be attributed to the present synthesis unless independent prior use is established:

- **mythology construction** in the complete semantic sense defined here;
- **endogenous mythology formation** for the distributed, substantially
  undesigned emergence and selection of mythology from changing conditions, and
  **purposive mythology construction** for the prospective research branch
  concerning target-state-guided intervention in that ecology;
- **mythology as a privileged symbolic intervention surface**, distinct from the
  whole culture-implementation stack;
- **mythologies as the assembly language of the bigOther** as the originating
  analogy for evaluated narrative units close to the classifier's learned
  operations; it does not imply literal digital computation or narrative
  sufficiency;
- **resonant mythology construction** as its recipient-fit component;
- **affective return to uphill development** for the non-deterministic transition
  from a self-reinforcing immediate-reward, dependency, or renewable-negation
  circuit into answerable agency, practice, contribution, repair, and renewed
  future possibility; together with **foreclosed flourishing**, **vocational
  shame**, and the four affective routes under their standing, attribution, and
  correction boundaries;
- **deservingness characteristic** for the attributed person-standing, need,
  contribution, sacrifice, entrusted responsibility, retained capability, or
  ascribed position offered as a reason for provision, protection, deference, or
  status; it is distinct from psychological entitlement, legal entitlement,
  deservingness judgment, and any scalar measure of human worth;
- **The Answerable Voice**, **The Road Back**, **The Better Song**, **The Stolen
  Inheritance**, **The Return to Vocation**, **Breaking the Velvet Chain**, and
  **The Guild of Builders** as compatible counter-weave crystallization cores,
  not independent complete Culture Programs or self-authenticating verdicts;
  ARC supplies the external “A Better Story” umbrella, while Better Song and the
  extension from narrative into affectively inhabitable culture are project
  developments;
- **The Child at Play** as the present person's affirmative capacity for wonder,
  risk, play, creation, and beginning again; the Nietzschean Camel--Lion--Child
  mapping, the Edelman counter-lineage, the present-child/concrete-child/successor
  ordering, and the claim that succession is consequence rather than silent
  principal are project constructions;
- **Mastery as play beyond predefined form, made possible by embodied
  discipline**, **The Child leaps. The Master shapes. The Guardian holds. The
  Successor begins again**, and the two-function Guardian of player and playfield;
  the received *Nur der Meister bricht die Form*, biblical material, and guardian
  angel are source images rather than authors of the project mapping;
- **vitalization** and **agonistic vitalization** at mythology level for the
  bounded process by which mythic radiance, humour, music, play, craft, contest,
  or festival makes a life-supporting path immediately attractive while forming
  longer-run capability and relation; the ecology-level definition of mythic
  radiance remains controlling and pleasure or applause supplies no drive verdict;
- the complementary **civilizational life-drive** and
  **wild-but-sublimated life-drive** actuation-mode distinction, together with
  **intrinsic practice goods** as the descriptive emphasis that expression,
  excellence, encounter, play, and love of a practice need not be exhausted by
  political instruction; these are not new drive poles, psychic substances,
  constitutional orientations, or proofs of life-serving direction;
- the **lifted sublimation-generator pattern**, **lifted sublimation generator**,
  and **positive sublimation generator**, including the distinction among
  generator kernel, actuator, formative architecture, and reflective audit;
- **duty as first sublimation and continuing infrastructure** in its present
  architectural rather than necessarily chronological sense;
- **Humanity-as-standing**, **Humanity-as-horizon**,
  **Humanity-as-sacred-object**, the combined **Humanity mythology**, and
  **Humanity inversion**;
- **pattern-principal displacement**, the formulation **the pattern of
  differences can become the higher good**, and the custody pathway compressed
  as **metric custody becomes policy custody**, together with the distinction
  among explicit metrics, tacit operational criteria, and pseudo-metric
  predication; the latter's primary linguistic home is the linguistic inventory;
- the **integrated doctrine-generation and authorization pathway**, including
  its separate construction and runtime orders, the compact formulation
  “a master signifier activates; a causal story attributes; a mythology
  legitimates; and an institution implements,” the Minogue--Stone--Sowell--
  Horowitz synthesis, the corrigible-versus-closed branch, and the three-level
  Humanism--Diversity and wetland application; the component concepts retain
  their existing semantic homes, and the synthesis establishes neither
  *Willkür* nor a named application without the additional gate;
- **experimental bounded authorization** for first exposing an unproven causal
  theory and remedy at the smallest feasible rights-preserving failure domain
  under predeclared comparison, stop, rollback, compensation, repair, and
  scaling rules; the generic controller remains in *Epistemic Constitution* and
  the concrete trial design in *Culture Program Ecology*;
- the typed distinction among **anti-humanist**, **anti-anthropocentric**,
  **anti-futurist**, **anti-continuation**, and **anti-person** positions as the
  project's source-audit structure; the external authors supply particular
  positions within the range, not the five-way audit itself;
- the **model-to-mandate custody chain**, **scientistic authorization** as a typed
  crossing rather than an umbrella accusation, **Teflon-authority insulation**,
  **prediction exposure**, **prediction ledger**, **decision--correction
  custody**, **surrogate-correction asymmetry**, and the typed
  **legal-surrogate branch**, including the Dutch nitrogen authorization-chain
  decomposition and the mechanisms' decision-scale, provenance, correction,
  attribution-ladder, and non-verdict boundaries;
- **aspirational exemplar** and the five-part distinction among domain,
  character, constructed or fictional, civilizational, and near exemplars;
  ***Leuchtturm*** is the originating canonical analogy for this construct;
- **Kantian and Randian generator configurations** as the comparative pattern
  defined here;
- the **relevant-structure resonance hypothesis** and
  **relevant-structure-weaponization schema** in their present Culture Mechanics
  forms;
- the **change-indexed moral-mobilization**, **mirrored-relevance**, and
  **foregrounded-relevance** hypotheses as named and bounded here;
- **finite operational demand** for a specified, testable, and terminable
  project-level demand, contrasted with both open desire and a renewable demand;
  the term and three-way analytical distinction are project-developed even though
  desire and demand are Lacanian source terms;
- the **critique–construction asymmetry hypothesis** for programmes whose
  practices of grievance discovery, delegitimation, unbraiding, and mobilization
  become more operationally developed than their practices of construction,
  maintenance, correction, succession, and stopping; and
  **negative-operational displacement** for the subordinate pathway in which
  those concrete negative operations outlive or displace an underspecified
  positive telos;
- **Ragnarök trajectory**, canonically defined in [*Epistemic Constitution of
  Lifted Mechanical
  Solidarity*](0030-epistemic-constitution.semantic-definition.md#ragnarok-trajectory),
  for a death-drive temporal path in which successful programme reproduction
  advances terminal rupture and possible self-extinction; this section applies
  the term to mythology reproduction without making transmission inherently
  life-drive;
- the **projective-vandalism circuit** for the originating sequence
  “externalize the blemish → strike the blemish → enjoy the strike,” and
  **denunciatory jouissance** as its candidate repetitive reward mechanism;
  neither term establishes projection, gratification, or pathology from an
  extreme accusation alone;
- **pre-articulation genesis** and the five-phase distinction among genesis,
  articulation, sacralization, institutionalization, and reproduction with field
  back-action;
- **receiver–order mismatch** for a condition in which an inherited order no
  longer accommodates important developing dispositions, relations, needs, or
  aspirations before a coherent successor has been articulated;
- **aspirational authorization** for authority acquired through experienced fit
  with receiver needs and aspirations rather than through epistemic warrant;
- **functional-remainder test** for identifying the need, aspiration, grievance,
  coordination problem, or protected good that remains after a doctrine's
  explicit explanation is criticized;
- **doctrinal-progeny test**, **offspring profile**, and **generative spirit** for
  evaluating a doctrine through the distribution and mechanism of its actual
  descendants rather than through literal propositions or preferred examples
  alone;
- the five-way relation among **entailment**, **authorization or affordance**,
  **selection**, **concealed causal design or undisclosed implementation**, and
  **appropriation or inversion**; the relation names are ordinary language, but
  the complete taxonomy and its use here are project-developed;
- **doctrinal lying by omission** as a moral verdict requiring supported knowledge
  and material non-disclosure, kept distinct from the descriptive category of
  concealed causal design;
- **recipient-architecture bifurcation** and **first-order recapture** as the
  conditional hypotheses that the same abstract doctrine can produce CPU-centred
  rule commitment, migration of finality to current outputs or interpreters, or
  non-uptake under different receiver and institutional conditions;
- **Herrschaftsideologie** in the narrow Culture Mechanics sense of a
  self-legitimating privileged-vision mythology that converts claimed epistemic
  asymmetry into governing jurisdiction; the German compound is pre-existing,
  while this definition and mechanism are project-developed;
- **revolutionary-overshoot hypothesis** and **mobilizational absoluteness** as the
  proposed mechanism and independent dimension defined above;
- **Mythology Construction Toolkit**;
- **one-slot hypothesis** or **one-PCI-slot hypothesis** for exclusive occupancy by
  one local bigOther executing one operative weave, including compatible-package
  compositionality and unbraid/rebraid replacement under incompatibility;
- **mythology as braid**, **rope of logic**, **incumbent-braid map**, and **successor-braid map**;
- **braiding**, **unbraiding**, and **rebraiding** as the construction, dissolution, and replacement operations applied to mythology;
- **situated cultural script**, **script viability**, **script
  load-bearingness**, and **cross-script cascade**, inherited from their canonical
  definition in [*Culture
  Mechanics*](0010-culture-mechanics.semantic-definition.md#situated-cultural-scripts-executable-local-braids),
  together with **script-and-interface map** and **bridge script** as the present
  toolkit's diagnostic and transition applications;
- the three-way distinction among local **script repair**, compatible **script
  replacement**, and governing **cultural unbraiding and rebraiding**;
- **mythological apologetics** and **mythological polemics** as the generalized functions defined here;
- **mythological rivalry**, **mythological conflict**, and **cultural-program
  conflict** as the replacement-centered distinctions defined here;
- **rival-signal suppression** and **counter-program suppression** as analytical
  names that do not adopt the incumbent program's description of opposition as
  random noise;
- **apologetic slot**, **polemical slot**, and **systemic slot** as positions generated by a solidarity order;
- **actor–slot coupling** as the fit among disposition, preparation, symbolic credibility, recognition, and systemic function;
- **systemic actor selection** and the **natural-choice hypothesis** for how solidarity orders differentially recruit and ratify their characteristic defenders and attackers;
- **solidarity force field** as the E–M-field-inspired analogy joining mythological roles, social gradients, actor gravitation, and reciprocal field modification;
- **institutional apologetic actor** and **institutional polemical actor** for offices or organizations whose agency persists across individual representatives;
- **authority migration** from personal embodiment in first-order solidarity toward persistent office and institution in lifted solidarity;
- **actor–slot map** as a toolkit output;
- **mythological replacement cycle**: map → preserve → unbraid → bridge → rebraid → maintain;
- **trusted-opinion-leader capture followed by trusted-channel rebraiding** as the
  human-carrier form of the covert attack pattern in which an adversary gains
  influence through authenticated internal leaders and uses their authority to
  help install one successor weave; offices, institutions, appointment systems,
  and channels can be captured analogously without being classified as leaders;
- Benda's ***clerc*** remains his source-attested intellectual-vocation role; its
  relation to trusted opinion leader as a functional receiving-field position is
  a Culture Mechanics mapping. Their overlap can add moral and epistemic
  authorization to a trusted-channel cascade, but neither role establishes
  betrayal or covert capture;
- **sentinel inversion** for the limiting case in which a recognized guardian
  becomes the authenticated delivery channel for rival programming;
- **apologetic closure**, **polemical corrosion**, and **polemical vacancy**;
- **cynicism as corrosive acid** on an installed mythology;
- the **Freud–Bernays unbraiding/rebraiding analogy**;
- **culture mechanics** as the framework's descriptive counterpart to mythology construction;
- **epistemic agency** and **epistemic constitution** in the specific operational senses defined here; the words have broader uses elsewhere, but no single external definition is being imported;
- **epistemic constitution of lifted mechanical solidarity**;
- **adversarial linguistic audit** as the framework's combined inspection procedure;
- **three-component resonance model**;
- **observable resonance surface** in the specified multidimensional form;
- **cultural-meta-environment hypothesis**;
- **mythology family**;
- **producer–guardian model**, **egalitarian guardian**, and the proposed producer–guardian genetic-cluster hypothesis; the producer/guardian labels are loosely inspired by [Plato's *Republic*](https://classics.mit.edu/Plato/republic.html), but the model itself is not Plato's;
- **psychological gradient** as paired with drive direction;
- **psychologically downhill mythology** and **psychologically uphill mythology** in the present sense;
- **downhill honey trap**;
- **bundle of shared sublimations**;
- **cultural sublimation architecture** for the durable mythology-bearing
  arrangement that recurrently selects, teaches, rewards, embodies, and transmits
  shared sublimations;
- **generative centrality** for the degree to which a structure produces
  capabilities or conditions upon which several downstream structures depend,
  kept distinct from felt relevance and normative standing;
- **sublimation emblem** as an expressly Wegner-facing explanatory shorthand for
  a symbolic carrier's condensed representative function, not a new canonical
  culture-mechanical object type;
- the **sublimation-carrier activation**,
  **counter-mythological-reprogramming**, and
  **generative-centrality/relevance-mismatch** hypotheses in their bounded,
  testable forms;
- **mythology as education of psychological taste**;
- **coupling amplitude**, **coupling radius**, and **coupling thermostat** as social-mythological analogies;
- **random crowd**, **clockwork crowd**, and **laser crowd**, the simple
  coordination--synchronization continuum, the Durkheimian correspondence, and the
  claim that a laser moment can regenerate the moral protocol used by a clockwork
  system;
- **dormant**, **de-animated**, and **culturally lost** as the project-developed
  three-way distinction among inactive living mythology, affectively dead
  remnants, and loss of a viable cultural transmission and activation system;
- **marriage-mediated coupling-range hypothesis**;
- **genetic-hardware hypothesis** for lifted mechanical solidarity;
- **first-order mechanical solidarity** and **lifted mechanical solidarity**;
- **first-order full capture**, **lifted full capture**, the distinction between
  shared answers and a shared answer generator, and **universal jurisdiction over
  the allocation and limitation of first-order outputs and jurisdictions**,
  inherited from the coordinated theory project and applied to mythology
  construction here;
- **cultural interoperability**, **effective meta-order thickness**,
  **lifted-solidarity surface area / meta-order span**, and **effective shared
  surface**;
- **cultural-interoperability floor (anti-Babel floor)**, **thin lifted mechanical
  solidarity**, **nominal lifted meta-order**, and **operative shared lifted
  mechanical solidarity**;
- **intergroup semantic decoupling** and **cultural interoperability breakdown
  (Babelization)** as local mythological synchronization combined with loss of
  society-wide translation, legitimate common procedure, and coordination;
- the inherited **un-reason--Babelization bridge**: un-reason can make reality
  causally common but no longer epistemically common around a protected account
  and can contribute to intergroup semantic decoupling, but localized closure is
  not itself cultural interoperability breakdown;
- the inherited **secession-or-force fork**: where severe Babelization leaves an
  unavoidable binding output without mutually legitimate adjudication, the
  jurisdiction must be separated or one answer imposed; separation is the only
  non-overpowering exit, while the soft form can preserve a possibility of
  restored interoperability and voluntary reunion; the detailed political
  relation and *Machtpolitik* boundary belong to *Culture Program Ecology*;
- **residual interoperability scale**, with **Babelization grain size** as
  intuitive shorthand when scale is material; **very-small-group Babelization**
  as the micro-island condition; and **individual Babelization** as its limiting
  case rather than the definition of individuality;
- the **loneliness-as-interoperability-consequence hypothesis** as a possible
  downstream experiential sensor requiring separation from objective isolation
  and rival demographic, material, technological, and clinical causes;
- **collective-agency failure** as a possible downstream effect rather than a
  synonym for interoperability breakdown;
- **culture mixing as one Babel mechanism** under failed second-order integration;
- **reality-coupled mythology** as an explicit design category;
- **individual-sovereignty technology suite**;
- **adversarial mythology construction**;
- **dark artist** under the strict conduct-based definition given here;
- **qualified-concept alarm**, **semantic wrapper**, and **operational payload** as an adversarial semantic audit;
- **Perception–Context–Permission** as the causal-action routing sequence;
- the smoke-alarm maxim joining visible destruction, mythological analysis, and causal attribution.

### 43. Scope and ethical cautions

1. **Mythology does not mean falsehood.** A mythology can preserve truth, error, fiction, sacred commitment, and adaptive practical knowledge in one cultural form.
2. **Construction does not imply total control.** Recipients interpret, resist, combine, parody, and transform what they receive.
3. **Resonance does not establish truth or benefit.** It predicts cultural fit, not reality correspondence or moral worth.
4. **Failure to resonate does not establish falsity.** A true account may lack intelligible cultural form or demand an acquired taste.
5. **Genetic influence is probabilistic, polygenic, and population-sensitive.** It does not determine an individual and must not be inferred mechanically from group membership.
6. **Genetic hypotheses remain inside the theory.** Their current measurement limits define a research programme; they are not grounds for silently deleting the proposed mechanism.
7. **Population comparison requires rigorous design.** Score portability, stratification, indirect genetic effects, measurement invariance, and environmental difference must be addressed.
8. **Recipient adaptation is not a licence to deceive.** Compatible variants must converge on compatible operations and outcomes.
9. **Moral reframing is not automatically manipulation.** It becomes deceptive when it hides material contradiction, invents evidence, or promises incompatible worlds.
10. **Psychologically uphill does not mean maximally painful.** Difficulty is constructive only when it serves capability, relationship, creation, maintenance, truth, or posterity.
11. **Pleasure and ease are not inherently death-driven.** Rest, play, affection, and mastered virtue can replenish life or make good practice habitual.
12. **Sublimation is not identical to repression.** The framework asks how energy is transformed and whether the resulting form supports flourishing.
13. **Diversity is not cultural interoperability breakdown (Babelization).** The
    breakdown concerns loss of operative shared translation, legitimate common
    procedure, and coordination below the anti-Babel floor; differentiated unity
    can sustain extensive first-order diversity above that floor. Residual
    interoperability scale does not measure human worth or individuality, and
    loneliness is neither the definition nor self-authenticating evidence of
    Babelization.
14. **Democratic mismatch does not establish civilizational inferiority.** It predicts that institutions require cultural and dispositional coadaptation.
15. **Visible destruction is evidence for investigation, not proof of a chosen cause.** Rival causal stories must be tested.
16. **Dark artist is not a partisan label.** It requires demonstrable strategic conduct, knowledge, and causal responsibility.
17. **Deterrence is not vengeance.** Legitimate costs require evidence, proportionality, jurisdiction, and due process.
18. **Constructive mythology remains corrigible.** A mythology that exempts itself from its own epistemic safeguards has begun to become a dark art.
19. **The one-slot hypothesis is an architectural claim, not literal
    neuroanatomy.** People can hold multiple schemas, identities, stories, and
    represented alternatives, while one operative lens can braid several compatible
    packages. The claim denies parallel governing bigOthers and runtime ranking;
    incompatible governing capture requires rejection or unbraid/rebraid
    replacement.
20. **Polemics is not permission to lie or dehumanize.** Legitimate unbraiding attacks demonstrable claims, bindings, authority relations, practices, and consequences.
21. **Apologetics is not immunity from correction.** Defence becomes closure when every objection is reclassified as hostility and no evidence can loosen a strand.
22. **Negative critique is not automatically nihilistic.** Its quality depends on its evidence, feasible comparison, preserved goods, disclosed successor, and responsibility for transition costs.
23. **The Freud–Bernays symmetry is an analogy, not a complete causal history.** It clarifies complementary operations without collapsing psychoanalysis into deprogramming or propaganda into applied psychoanalysis.
24. **Natural choice is not predestination.** It predicts differential fit and recognition among available candidates, not one genetically destined officeholder.
25. **Systemic selection need not have a conscious selector.** “The system chooses” is shorthand for distributed attraction, filtering, training, appointment, imitation, reward, sanction, and collective recognition.
26. **Recognition does not establish truth or legitimacy.** A solidarity order can select gifted defenders of a false, corrupt, or death-driven mythology.
27. **Institutional agency does not erase human responsibility.** Institutions persist through offices and procedures, but identifiable humans still decide, speak, enforce, resist, and bear responsibility for their conduct.
28. **Self-appointment and self-preparation are different.** A person may deliberately prepare and compete for a role; what cannot be privately manufactured is the recognized systemic authority attached to the slot.
29. **Full, lifted, universal, and corrigible are not synonyms.** Full describes
    complete slot occupancy; lifted means that shared binding force lies in the
    generator rather than the generated concrete answers; universal describes how
    far that generator governs; and corrigible describes openness to inspection,
    succession, and reality's right of refusal. Rival construction or replacement
    is not pathological merely because it is rival.
30. **Changed belief is not evidence of covert attack.** Capture of a trusted
    opinion leader or an institutional channel requires evidence of provenance,
    dependency, coordination, concealment, or control that defeats serious
    alternatives such as sincere conversion, intellectual development, new
    evidence, ordinary incentives, generational succession, and independent
    convergence. Longitudinal objective substitution can justify a broader
    system-level Culture Program capture investigation before a covert commander
    is identified; it does not by itself justify actor-specific command
    attribution.
31. **This toolkit is mythology-specific, not the definitive complete
    culture-implementation manual.** Use *Systems Leadership* for the exact
    practical method. Mythology has an elevated place as the bigOther's assembly
    language, but systems, symbols, leadership behaviour, habits, and repeated
    experience may be equally necessary or more decisive in an implementation.
32. **Mythology is not always historically first.** A doctrine can initially
    articulate prior structural and practical change and only later become an
    upstream programming force.
33. **Aspirational authorization is not epistemic or constitutional warrant.**
    Receiver fit can explain authority without vindicating truth, legitimacy,
    jurisdiction, or consequence.
34. **Revolutionary overshoot is not a licence to deceive.** It is a comparative
    hypothesis about selection and displacement under incumbent resistance; its
    conscious use remains answerable to truthfulness, proportionality,
    jurisdiction, foreseeable harm, and the availability of less destructive
    means.
35. **Mobilizational absoluteness does not classify order level.** Categorical
    language can defend either first-order answers or a lifted generator and can
    accompany open or closed, differentiated or absorptive orders.
36. **Endogenous does not mean good or intervention-free.** It identifies
    distributed causal formation, which can include inherited power and the
    accumulated effects of earlier designs.
37. **A chosen destination is not historical destiny or constitutional
    authority.** Stating \(D^*\) makes the constructor's end auditable; it neither
    proves that the baseline is wrong nor licenses intervention over every
    recipient and jurisdiction.
38. **The possibility of purposive construction is not a claim of sovereign
    engineering.** Any attempted intervention would remain subject to receiver
    interpretation, institutional selection, rival action, unintended consequence,
    and reality. The phrase *memetic engineering* names a neighboring control
    aspiration, not an achieved precision science.
39. **Descendants do not automatically convict ancestors.** Entailment,
    authorization, selection, concealed causal design, and appropriation must be
    distinguished, and common templates can make cases less independent than they
    appear.
40. **Literal innocence is not causal innocence.** A doctrine may omit the
    bigOther-facing and institutional operation on which its predictable effect
    depends; but knowledge, foreseeability, materiality, and non-disclosure must be
    established before calling the omission a lie.
41. **Receiver architecture is not a moral rank.** Low affective uptake can support
    reflective rule commitment, but it can also produce non-uptake, exploitation,
    incapacity for trust, or fragmentation.
42. **Strong *Beseelung* is not first-order recapture.** A lifted generator can be
    fully occupied and affectively authoritative while binding its interpreters to
    bounded jurisdiction, succession, and reality correction.
43. ***Herrschaftsideologie* is not legitimate expertise.** The pathology is the
    conversion of claimed superior perception into governing jurisdiction that
    exceeds its evidence, task, and correctable office.
44. ***Herrschaftsideologie* is not confined to one faction.** Apply its
    privileged-vision and jurisdiction tests symmetrically to incumbent and
    dissident, religious and secular, public and private authority.
45. **Culture Mechanics does not authorize a caste of culture engineers.**
    Understanding a proposed programming mechanism supplies no standing to operate
    on people without disclosed ends, bounded authority, refusal, oversight, and
    correction.
46. **Structural efficacy does not establish positive sublimation.** Persecution,
    obedient self-erasure, and technically accomplished falsehood can all be
    disciplined products of an effective generator.
47. **Duty is not established as the chronologically first sublimation.** Its
    “first” position in the proposed pattern is architectural; attachment,
    imitation, language, trust, turn-taking, and delayed gratification are serious
    developmental alternatives or prerequisites.
48. **Human standing is not earned by achievement.** A horizon of human
    possibility may orient aspiration, but proximity to a *Leuchtturm* does not
    increase dignity.
49. **Humanity is not a self-authorizing collective agent.** Invoking its future
    or true interest does not appoint an interpreter or license sacrifice of its
    concrete bearers.
50. **An exemplar has no automatic jurisdiction.** Demonstrated excellence,
    admiration, and inspiration in one domain do not prove a worldview or confer
    moral, epistemic, or political authority elsewhere.
51. **Declaring reason sovereign does not make it so.** Kantian and Randian
    configurations alike remain subject to the question whether conscious reason
    audited an operative verdict or served as its press secretary.
52. **Kant is not claimed as the historical inventor of the pattern.** His work
    supplies an unusually explicit configuration and source language; priority
    and causal influence require separate historical evidence.
53. **Un-reason is not Babelization.** One self-sealing argument or
    domain-specific failure of joint inquiry does not establish that translation,
    legitimate common procedure, and required coordination have crossed the
    society-wide or intergroup anti-Babel threshold.
54. **Babelization does not make every disagreement a secession-or-force
    choice.** The fork additionally requires incompatible binding outputs, a
    decision that cannot be avoided, and failure of mutually legitimate common
    adjudication. Compromise remains outside the fork while it is voluntary and
    commonly authorized.
55. **The Grail question does not answer itself.** “Serve” may refer to declared
    purpose, intended user, owner, immediate beneficiary, institutional incentive,
    competitive selection pressure, programme reproduction, or long-run
    trajectory, and these may diverge. Capability and abundance do not establish
    life-drive; harm does not establish one hidden beneficiary, conspiracy, AI
    consciousness, literal Moloch, destructive sublimation, un-reason, or a
    Ragnarök trajectory without their separate evidence.

## Semantic summary

- **Broader class:** cultural formation, reproduction, and prospective
  intervention.
- **Central object:** a connected mythology of causal stories, moral
  classifications, identities, roles, and expectations, together with its
  mythology-bearing relations to symbols, practices, systems, and institutions.
- **Elevated theoretical role:** mythologies are the **assembly language of the
  bigOther**--a privileged evaluated narrative interface, not the whole
  culture-implementation stack and not a sufficient cause by itself.
- **Immediate cultural mechanism:** training and activation of the mythological lens and bigOther.
- **Situated implementation layer:** the general braid becomes executable through
  distributed, role-, institution-, and situation-indexed cultural scripts that
  connect selected meanings and authorities to detailed routines, interfaces,
  improvisation, and practical support.
- **Script-replacement distinction:** local script repair or replacement can
  occur within the incumbent cultural braid; failure of load-bearing or mutually
  dependent scripts can form a cross-script cascade and contribute to wider
  cultural unbraiding.
- **Optional motivational layer:** constitutive lack can be modelled as a
  persistent motivational gradient; desire supplies open movement, fantasy
  organizes desirable objects and subject positions, mythology can supply
  culturally available material, finite demands give desire terminable vectors,
  and jouissance can reinforce a repetitive circuit. None of these terms replaces
  processor duality, slot occupancy, *Beseelung*, or the distributed field.
- **Programme-reproduction boundary:** authorship, teaching, institutional
  maintenance, and transmission are not inherently life-drive. In a Ragnarök
  trajectory they can make a terminal death-drive programme more capable until
  the containing order and possibly the programme itself lose continuity.
- **Service-direction audit:** Pageau's Grail question supplies a source bridge
  for asking whom or what produced capability serves. The project distinguishes
  declared and effective beneficiaries, operative objectives, sacrificed goods
  and substrates, mastery reversal, correction, and temporal trajectory before
  assigning drive valence or diagnosing destructive sublimation.
- **Critical asymmetry hypothesis:** positive aims can coexist with a more
  concrete, repeatable, and rewarded machinery of grievance, delegitimation, and
  unbraiding; negative-operational displacement is a possible pathway, not a
  definition of Critical Theory, the political left, or criticism as such.
- **Integrated doctrine-generation pathway:** a condition can be selected and
  attributed through a causal story, legitimated through mythology, compressed
  and activated through a master signifier, routed through PCP, and implemented
  through an authorization payload and carrying structure. The generic sequence
  is not *Willkür*; the classification requires additional custody,
  affected-person-standing, prospective-constraint, and correction failures.
- **Three-level application:** the abstract mechanism is tested through the
  Humanism--Diversity relation and a hypothetical wetland case while existing
  definitions of Humanism, Strong Programmatic Diversity, metric custody, silent
  principal, drive direction, and constitutional hazard remain controlling.
- **Source-attested anti-human boundary:** MacCormack supplies explicit
  anti-continuation, Edelman symbolic anti-futurity and a death-drive limit case,
  and Braidotti affirmative posthuman displacement. They are different
  positions, not one lineage, programme, or automatic Diversity classification.
- **Minogue source boundary:** the grievance-to-post-utopian-critique sequence is
  a project reconstruction of *Alien Powers*. Its “suicide pact” and “attempt to
  destroy humanity” conclusions are two distinct attributed terminal judgments,
  not one exact sentence, proof of conscious intent, or a self-authenticating
  death-drive or Ragnarök classification.
- **Two substantive research paths:** endogenous mythology formation describes
  distributed, substantially undesigned plant-like growth and selection; the
  prospective purposive-construction branch asks how far intervention guided by a
  declared destination \(D^*\) can work.
- **Intervention hypothesis boundary:** mythology may be a privileged symbolic
  surface for redirecting classification, but it is not the whole
  culture-implementation stack or a sovereign control interface.
- **Prospective outcome constraint:** where intervention is attempted, intention,
  receiver fit, incumbent resistance, institutional selection, rival intervention,
  unintended consequence, and reality jointly shape the actual successor.
- **Historical directionality:** mythology can begin as an imperfect output of
  structural change and diffuse aspiration, then become an input through
  sacralization, institutionalization, reproduction, and field back-action.
- **Pre-articulation condition:** receiver–order mismatch names developing
  dispositions, relations, needs, or aspirations that the inherited order no
  longer accommodates before a successor is coherently formulated.
- **Authorization distinction:** aspirational authorization explains authority
  through receiver fit without conferring epistemic truth, moral legitimacy, or
  constitutional jurisdiction.
- **Residual-function audit:** the functional-remainder test asks which real need
  or coordination problem survives criticism of the doctrine that carried it.
- **Doctrinal-progeny audit:** a doctrine's recurring descendants form an
  offspring profile that supplies defeasible evidence about its generative spirit;
  entailment, affordance, selection, concealed causal design, and appropriation
  remain distinct relations.
- **Implementation-integrity rule:** CPU-facing doctrine must be compared with the
  bigOther-facing classifications and institutional payload on which its actual
  operation depends; supported knowing non-disclosure can constitute doctrinal
  lying by omission.
- **Recipient-architecture bifurcation:** the same abstract doctrine can support
  CPU-centred rule commitment, first-order recapture, or non-uptake under different
  affective, reflective, institutional, crisis, and power conditions.
- ***Herrschaftsideologie*:** a privileged-vision mythology converts claimed
  epistemic asymmetry into governing jurisdiction and tends to close correction by
  treating disagreement as proof of the ruled person's incapacity.
- **Recipient-fit component:** resonant mythology construction.
- **Relevant-structure resonance hypothesis:** mythologies often gain a selection
  advantage when recipients believe that they recognize, support, or offer an
  honourable role in felt-relevant structures; perceived support, actual
  multidimensional effects, and normative standing remain separate.
- **Relevance pathways:** change-indexing may intensify mobilization; foregrounding
  may activate installed relevance; mirroring may temporarily extend felt
  relevance through identification; repeated reinforcement is required before
  durable relevance reprogramming can be inferred.
- **Cultural sublimation architecture:** the durable interlocking mythologies,
  limits, exemplars, roles, practices, systems, rewards, sanctions, institutions,
  and transmission pathways that recurrently transform drives into character,
  capability, relationship, creation, maintenance, and posterity.
- **Generative-centrality distinction:** a sublimation architecture can be
  causally load-bearing for many downstream structures while receiving little
  felt relevance; inheritance, dependency, actual consequence, and normative
  standing require separate evidence.
- **Lifted sublimation-generator pattern:** an installed second-order rule-set
  generates binding directions, an actuator gives them motivational force, and a
  formative architecture converts them into durable character, capability,
  relationship, practice, role, or institution.
- **Duty's proposed dual position:** rule-bound self-command can be a first
  architectural sublimation and remain continuing infrastructure beneath richer
  self-authored capability; this does not establish chronological priority.
- **Generator-valence distinction:** formative effectiveness is neutral; a
  positive generator must additionally be life-supporting, person-preserving,
  capability-producing, reality-answerable, and corrigible.
- **Humanity triad:** Humanity-as-standing gives every concrete person complete
  non-instrumental standing; Humanity-as-horizon presents plural human
  possibilities; Humanity-as-sacred-object gives the standing affective
  protection.
- **Humanity inversion:** an abstraction meant to protect persons becomes an
  alleged collective subject whose privileged interpreters may consume those
  persons in its name.
- **Pattern-principal displacement:** a selected pattern of differences becomes
  the protected principal and its concrete bearers become carriers, inputs, or
  obstacles; metric custody can become policy custody when one interpreter also
  controls deficit, remedy, sacrifice, success, completion, and appeal. Where no
  stable operational rule exists, pseudo-metric language may instead imitate the
  authority of measurement and join master-signifier custody directly to
  discretionary policy custody. These are surrogate/*Willkür* affordances, not
  automatic diagnoses of capture, death-drive, manipulation, or
  *Willkürherrschaft*. The assistant-proposed and author-selected `Mθ`
  constructed-mapping schematic is explanatory notation for the custody choices,
  not a claim that every metric is scalar, formally differentiable, or captured
  by one validated mathematical model.
- **Aspirational exemplars:** *Leuchttürme* make valued human possibilities
  visible; near exemplars and pathways can translate distant aspiration into
  practice, while excellence supplies no automatic jurisdiction.
- **Rival generator configurations:** Kant binds cultivation to universal law,
  Humanity, and duty; Rand binds disciplined character to rational life and
  flourishing. The comparison supplies reciprocal diagnostics rather than
  collapsing their ethics.
- **Reflective audit gate:** a positive generator should help produce people able
  to distinguish press-secretary justification, interpretation, and genuine
  audit of rule, framing, provenance, authority, consequence, and corrigibility.
- **Symbolic-condensation bridge:** a sublimation emblem is useful Wegner-facing
  shorthand for a carrier that stands in for a larger formative machinery; in
  canonical terms the carrier activates installed lens mappings rather than
  containing the architecture or adding a decoder.
- **Counter-mythological pathway:** rival mythology can reclassify a sublimated
  practice; repeated symbolic, institutional, and social reinforcement can
  reprogram the mythological lens, change local-bigOther sentiment, and alter
  Beseelung, enactment, and transmission. De-animation alone does not establish
  deliberate attack or justified preservation.
- **Relevant-structure weaponization:** Wegner's *Vierschritt der Kontrolle*
  provides an upstream schema for presenting one's side as structural support and
  a rival as damage or tolerated damage. Hanekamp's ecological formulation
  supplies the applied bridge from descriptive change through represented
  weakening to deterioration; use of either schema is not by itself proof of
  deceit, actual damage, or legitimate intervention.
- **Applied component:** the present provisional Mythology Construction Toolkit,
  subordinate to the definitive whole-culture implementation method in *Systems
  Leadership*; the complete on-purpose design, release, monitoring, and redesign
  lifecycle remains future work.
- **Resonance substrate:** species-typical human machinery plus heritable dispositional configuration.
- **Cultural-meta-environment:** distributed mythologies, institutions, peers, media, and public classifications that can produce patterned non-genetic formation beyond the shared household.
- **Observable interface:** expressed dispositions × moral intuitions × motives ×
  relevant structures × causal worldview × group identity × authority structure ×
  institutions × situation.
- **Construction grammar:** topoi, plot, causal stories, characters, sacred values, master signifiers, identity offers, role offers, and PCP.
- **Recipient reconstruction:** finite messages are compressed against assumptions
  about recipient knowledge and programming; construction must audit the complete
  factual, causal, moral, and authorization model recipients reconstruct, not only
  the literal message.
- **Political authorization:** Wegner's *Talking Points* maxim treats political
  language as a means of winning trust and power over people's lives; Culture
  Mechanics translates this as an authorization bid. Where PCP ends in delegated
  power, inspect its scope, jurisdiction, duration, discretion, oversight,
  revocability, termination rule, and epistemic spillover.
- **Construction topology:** cross-bound strands of story, moral judgment, identity, symbol, role, ritual, institution, and material practice form a redundant rope of logic.
- **Single-slot constraint:** one local bigOther executes one operative mythological weave; no higher runtime process ranks parallel governing bigOthers.
- **Full-capture distinction:** a first-order program and a lifted meta-program can each completely occupy the one slot; fullness does not by itself imply closure.
- **Order-level distinction:** first-order solidarity shares concrete rules and answers; lifted solidarity shares rules that generate, select, apply, and revise concrete answers.
- **Lifted jurisdiction:** a shared answer generator may govern universally while constituting, allocating, limiting, coordinating, and governing the succession and correction of first-order outputs and jurisdictions.
- **Package compositionality:** several compatible religious, professional, familial, national, or local packages can be constituent strands of that one weave.
- **Replacement problem:** an incompatible governing mythology cannot be installed beside the incumbent; the incumbent must be unbraided sufficiently for one successor to be rebraided, installed, and made authoritative.
- **Defensive operation:** mythology-level apologetics explains, repairs, and renews the braid.
- **Offensive operation:** mythology-level polemics isolates and loosens false or destructive bindings while preserving recoverable strands.
- **Conflict transition:** mythological rivalry becomes mythological conflict when
  participants actively interfere with a rival's transmission, authority,
  membership, or institutional embodiment; incompatible governing lenses create
  cultural-program conflict.
- **Actor question:** apologetics and polemics are performed by persons, offices, or institutions recognized by the relevant solidarity order.
- **Authority scaling:** first-order solidarity tends toward personalized actors; lifted solidarity can locate durable agency in offices and institutions represented by replaceable humans.
- **Authority boundary:** the lifted meta-order is universal at its level, while its representative persons, offices, and institutions remain bounded and receive no ownership of all interpretation or reality.
- **Selection mechanism:** systemic slot × dispositional fit × preparation × collective or institutional ratification → actor–slot coupling.
- **Reciprocal field effect:** the solidarity field selects its characteristic actors, and those actors reinforce, redirect, or destabilize the field.
- **Entry question:** Why would this population recognize and adopt the mythology?
- **Momentum question:** Which path does the mythology make psychologically downhill or uphill?
- **Destination question:** Does repetition move toward life-drive creation or death-drive sterility and destruction?
- **Formative mechanism:** sublimation and education of psychological taste.
- **Durable implementation:** ritual, practice, selection, reward, sanction, institution, infrastructure, and memory.
- **Scale problem:** coupling strength and radius, random/clockwork/laser crowd
  modes, lifted solidarity, coordination, synchronization, cultural
  interoperability, and its breakdown (Babelization).
- **Coupling continuum:** coordination and synchronization are ideal-typical ends
  of an interaction-strength continuum; organic solidarity resembles clockwork
  coordination, mechanical solidarity resembles laser synchronization, and a
  laser moment can renew the moral protocol used by a clockwork system.
- **Democracy mismatch hypothesis:** imported civic operations may fail when their mythology does not fit the population's dispositional and potentially genetic hardware.
- **Interoperability implementation:** effective thickness × meta-order span →
  effective shared lifted-solidarity surface; the first-order proportionality
  model treats cultural interoperability as increasing with that surface.
- **Anti-Babel floor:** differentiated groups remain in one functioning shared
  order only while cultural interoperability stays above the required threshold.
- **Un-reason bridge:** when reasoning removes consequences and counterevidence
  from independent authority, the shared world can remain causally common while
  ceasing to be epistemically common. This can contribute to semantic decoupling;
  it becomes Babelization only at the wider interoperability threshold.
- **Political fork:** where severe Babelization removes legitimate adjudication
  at an unavoidable binding interface, the remaining branches are jurisdictional
  separation or imposed settlement. Separation is the only non-overpowering exit;
  a soft form can preserve the possibility of voluntary reunion. Power
  determining valid reasons and rules makes the force branch open domination;
  *Machtpolitik* requires the further strict composite conditions defined in
  *Culture Program Ecology*.
- **Culture-mixing threshold:** incompatible first-order mythologies can multiply
  or semantically decouple faster than operative shared lifted solidarity can gain
  thickness and span.
- **Long feedback loop:** potentially fast gene–culture coevolution through niche selection, institution, status, partner choice, migration, fertility, survival, and reproduction.
- **Positive design ideal:** reality-coupled, life-drive mythology that grows capability and preserves correction.
- **Affective-return mechanism:** psychologically downhill reward or dependency
  can be interrupted by concrete loss recognition, discriminating attribution,
  preserved standing, answerable responsibility, recoverable hope, bounded
  practice, mastery, belonging, repair, and succession. The sequence is a design
  hypothesis rather than a stage law, and affect supplies no causal or moral
  oracle.
- **Foreclosed flourishing:** an immediate benefit can displace capability-forming
  effort and substantially contract a plausible developmental path before its
  value becomes visible. Possessed-good, expected-future, possible-self, and
  capability losses remain distinct, and the counterfactual and causal path must
  be supported rather than supplied by grief or anger.
- **Recovery-literature correction:** painful discrepancy may interrupt a
  circuit, but a longer future, autonomous authorship, better immediate
  alternatives, capability, relationships, recovery capital, and corrigible
  failure make the uphill life inhabitable. This is a bounded transfer from a
  clinical source family, not a diagnosis of cultural adherence.
- **Provider-subject and deservingness audit:** mature interdependence keeps the
  recipient's standing and need visible while restoring the provider as another
  finite subject, other legitimate claims, production, maintenance, and possible
  reciprocity or transmission. A deservingness characteristic states the offered
  basis of allocation; it neither equals entitlement nor ranks human worth.
- **Affirmative counter-weave:** The Child at Play, Master, Guardian, Successor,
  and Better Song join formation, liberating refusal, creation, deep trust,
  bounded protection, mythic radiance, play, humour, craft, and succession. The
  present Child-function is primary, concrete children retain direct standing,
  and the successor horizon is downstream rather than a silent commanding
  principal.
- **Complementary actuation modes:** civilizational life-drive forms durable
  cooperation and accumulated capability; wild-but-sublimated life-drive supplies
  immediacy, courage, social energy, self-distance, and renewed desire through
  bounded play, art, humour, festivity, and rivalry. They remain modes inside one
  drive orientation, and neither solemnity nor pleasure supplies its own valence.
- **Counterculture completion:** “Against the tune, the theft, and the
  capture—not against the people being led”; the counterculture must be able to
  win and stop fighting, release its constitutive adversary, preserve roads back,
  and transfer honour from denunciation to answerable building and repair.
- **Primary adversarial threat:** psychologically downhill mythology using dark arts to create dependency, dissipate energy, and disable maturation.
- **Cognitive-relief hypothesis:** under complexity and uncertainty, a compressed
  authority-endorsed formula may gain adoption partly by relieving the burden of
  independent judgment; this is a testable pathway, not a claim about intelligence
  or all compression.
- **Semantic dark-art test:** strip the moral wrapper from qualified concepts and inspect the concrete permissions they authorize.
- **Carrier–replicator warning:** a mythology can reproduce successfully by inducing conduct that damages its human carriers.
- **Replacement sequence:** incumbent-braid and script map → preserve sound strands
  and viable bridge scripts → polemical unbraiding → liminal bridge → successor
  braid and scripts → reality-coupled apologetics.
- **Revolutionary displacement hypothesis:** incumbent resistance may select
  strategically, sincerely, or culturally overextended counter-mythologies whose
  achievements can later be translated into more discriminating institutions or
  lifted generators.
- **Independent rhetorical-sacred dimension:** mobilizational absoluteness
  measures categorical, eternal, exceptionless, sacred, or non-negotiable
  presentation without deciding field strength, order level, jurisdiction,
  corrigibility, or fusion.
- **Covert replacement threat:** trusted-opinion-leader capture or analogous
  capture of an authority structure can invert trusted guardians, obscure
  provenance through nested internal channels, and convert vertical authorization
  into horizontal field proof while rebraiding one successor. Where a Bendaian
  *clerc* also occupies the trusted position, the channel can add clerc-like moral
  and epistemic authorization; neither overlap, changed belief, nor disclosed
  payment alone is proof of capture.
- **Ecology handoff:** longitudinal program disharmony and objective substitution
  can support investigation of broader Culture Program capture before an external
  commander is found; the ecology-level diagnosis does not itself identify a
  covert operational agent.
- **Carrying-structure handoff:** finance, formation, amplification, logistics,
  protection, appointments, and succession can be mapped as capability-bearing
  infrastructure before any human carrier is accused; restriction requires a
  stronger causal nexus and person-preserving process.
- **Self-Attribution Presumption:** believe an authenticated, serious actor
  proclamation first as evidence of the actor's own operative allegiance or
  perceived authorization; then test cosmology, causation, scope, defeaters,
  internal contestation, recurrence, and organizational support separately.
- **Projective-vandalism circuit:** projected blemish → taboo-marked target
  surface → morally authorized symbolic strike → audience resonance → possible
  denunciatory jouissance → reality-independent repetition; the motivational
  sequence remains a hypothesis with instrumental and institutional rivals.
- **Defensive sequence:** apologetic repair → immunization → superior constructive attractor → recovery → containment and deterrence.
- **Accountability rule:** sanctions attach to demonstrable conduct and causal responsibility.
- **Central normative distinction:** cultural replication is not identical to human or civilizational flourishing.

## Example of use

> The campaign succeeded at message distribution but failed as **mythology construction**. Its causal story resonated with the audience and produced rapid identity formation, yet it offered no reality-facing role, converted frustration into renewable demand, intensified local synchronization at the expense of civic solidarity, and protected itself from correction. It was therefore replicatively successful but psychologically downhill, institutionally corrosive, and weakly reality-coupled.

## Compact formulas

Umbrella definition:

> **mythology construction research = endogenous formation and selection +
> prospective purposive design and release + comparison of intended and actual
> outcomes**

Historical genesis and back-action:

> **endogenous mythology formation: structure + institutions + practice +
> receiver dispositions → diffuse needs and aspirations → articulation →
> distributed selection → sacralization + institutionalization → later
> programming and field back-action ↺**

Prospective purposive trajectory intervention:

> **candidate model: chosen destination D* + diagnosed baseline → deliberate
> mythology and supporting-system intervention → reception and embodiment →
> observation, correction, defence, or retirement**

Intention--outcome boundary:

> **actual successor = intended destination × receiver fit × incumbent resistance
> × institutional selection × rival intervention × unintended consequence ×
> reality; joint constraint, not calibrated multiplication**

Doctrinal-progeny audit:

> **explicit doctrine + implicit authority, subjecthood, and correction grammar ×
> receiver architecture × interpreters, institutions, incentives, power, rivals,
> and conditions → descendants; descendant distribution → offspring profile →
> defeasible evidence about generative spirit**

Privileged-vision conversion:

> **claimed access to a higher subject's hidden true interest → authorized
> interpreter → epistemic asymmetry converted into jurisdiction → disagreement
> reclassified as incapacity → correction loop closes**
