Authorization and remainder:

> **receiver fit → possible aspirational authorization, not truth or legitimacy;
> refute explicit doctrine → identify remaining need or coordination problem →
> compare better reality-coupled vehicles**

Assembly-language relation:

> **evaluated stories encode roles + causes + values + permissions + consequences → mythologies train general lens mappings → bigOther executes those mappings; systems + symbols + habits + conduct + repeated experience supply or disrupt the implementation environment**

Situated-script embodiment:

> **operative cultural braid + role/institution/situation + practical environment
> → situated cultural script → enactment and consequence → reinforcement, repair,
> compatible script replacement, or unraveling; load-bearing cross-script cascade
> → possible cultural unbraiding**

Relevant-structure resonance:

> **felt-relevant structures × represented support or damage → possible mythology
> adoption, retention, enactment, and transmission; actual multidimensional
> effects + normative standing remain independently answerable to reality and
> evaluation**

Relevance activation and mobilization:

> **foregrounding or mirroring → possible relevance activation; represented
> change to felt-relevant structure → possible intensified moral mobilization;
> repetition + practice + sanction + institution → possible durable relevance
> reprogramming**

Cultural sublimation architecture:

> **raw drive → culturally organized limit + example + practice + reward →
> sublimated character and capability → downstream relationships and institutions
> → reproduction, correction, neglect, or consumption of the formative
> architecture ↺**

Lifted sublimation generator:

> **fixed meta-rules \(M\) + current knowledge \(K_t\) + situation \(C\) →
> binding direction \(R_t(C)\); raw drive + \(R_t(C)\) + actuation + formative
> practice → sublimated form; consequence + criticism + reality → retain,
> correct, unbraid, or rebraid**

Humanity and exemplars:

> **complete equal standing in every concrete person + partial plural realization
> of an open human horizon; *Leuchtturm* → visible possibility, not graded dignity
> or jurisdiction**

Reflective audit:

> **felt verdict → interpret operative rule → trace provenance → test framing,
> symmetry, evidence, authority, jurisdiction, and consequence → cut false
> finality → retain, correct, or rebraid**

Symbolic condensation and counter-mythological reprogramming:

> **carrier → installed sublimation-related lens mappings → local-bigOther
> classification and practice; rival mythology + repeated symbolic and
> institutional reinforcement → possible lens reprogramming → changed Beseelung,
> enactment, and transmission**

Wegner's control schema:

> **select relevant structure → present my side as support → present rival as
> damage or tolerated damage → moral classification → possible alignment,
> sanction, or authorization**

Recipient reconstruction and authorization:

> **communicator's recipient model → selected facts + omissions + sequence +
> symbols → message → recipient codebook and lens → reconstructed factual, causal,
> moral, and authorization model → inspect requested power, jurisdiction,
> duration, oversight, revocation, and epistemic spillover**

Doctrine generation and implementation:

> **condition → causal story attributes → mythology legitimates → master
> signifier compresses and activates → distributed bigOther installs → PCP routes
> permission → authorization payload → surrogate custody → institution implements
> → affected persons retain standing or become causes and obstacles → adverse
> consequence corrects or renews the programme**

Single-slot composition:

> **compatible packages and libraries → cross-binding within one operative weave; incompatible governing program → rejection or unbraid/rebraid replacement, never runtime ranking of parallel bigOthers**

Lifting and jurisdiction:

> **full capture by shared concrete answers → unbraid their claim to finality → rebraid shared M; fixed M + current K_t + situation C → binding R_t(C); M may govern universally while generating bounded first-order jurisdictions**

Mythological replacement:

> **map incumbent braid and scripts → preserve sound strands and viable bridge
> scripts → polemically unbraid false bindings → cross the liminal interval →
> rebraid the successor and test its scripts → maintain through corrigible
> apologetics**

Revolutionary overshoot:

> **incumbent resistance × receiver–order mismatch → possible strategic
> overshoot, sincere absolutization, or cultural selection of categorical variants
> → displacement → test whether later institutions preserve the achieved
> jurisdiction without preserving false or destructive excess**

Mythological conflict:

> **informal mythological rivalry → active interference with transmission,
> authority, membership, or institutional embodiment → mythological conflict →
> incompatible governing programs → cultural-program conflict**

Crowd modes and coupling:

> **random co-presence → clockwork differentiated coordination → laser
> synchronization; coupling thermostat = enough common orientation for action +
> enough independent perception for correction**

Braided durability:

> **story × moral judgment × identity × symbol × role × ritual × institution × consequence → flexible and redundant rope of logic**

Actor selection:

> **systemic slot × dispositional fit × preparation × symbolic credibility × recognition → effective apologetic or polemical actor**

Authority scaling:

> **first-order solidarity → authoritative person embodies the mythology**

> **lifted solidarity → universal meta-order persists through plural, bounded institutions and replaceable human representatives**

Force-field reciprocity:

> **solidarity field selects actor ↔ actor reinforces or modifies solidarity field**

Three-component resonance:

> **common human machinery × heritable dispositional configuration × mythological form → probability of adoption, retention, enactment, and transmission**

Action routing:

> **mythological lens → causal story → Perception → Context → Permission → action**

Downhill capture:

> **resonance → immediate reward → renewable demand → repetition → identity and dependency → rising exit cost**

Ragnarök trajectory:

> **effective programme reproduction → wider installation of terminal logic →
> maintenance and transmission become instruments of negation → substrate is
> consumed → containing order and possibly programme lose continuity**

Motivational routing:

> **optional constitutive lack → open desire → fantasy organizes desirability and
> subject position → mythology may supply objects, roles, and obstruction →
> finite operational demand and completion OR renewable demand → repetition and
> possible jouissance**

Life-drive formation:

> **resonance → chosen challenge → sublimation → capability and contribution → maintenance and posterity**

Social range:

> **coupling amplitude + coupling radius → topology of trust, mythology, and cooperation**

Cultural interoperability and its breakdown (Babelization):

> **effective meta-order thickness `T_L` × meta-order span `S_L` → effective
> shared surface $\Sigma_L$; $I \approx k\Sigma_L$; $I < I_{AB}$ → cultural interoperability
> breakdown (Babelization) → possible society-wide collective-agency failure**

Reality coupling:

> **mythology gives practice meaning → practice produces consequences → consequences correct mythology**

Adversarial defence:

> **repair through apologetics + expose and unbraid through polemics + inoculate against the trick + build a superior attractor + preserve exit + impose conduct-based costs**

Covert trusted-opinion-leader threat:

> **map trusted opinion leader, possible clerc-like authorization, and supporting
> authority structures → hidden
> influence or selection → preserve internal carrier identity → translate through
> incumbent language → nested cascade + visible peer uptake → institutionalize →
> rebraid one naturalized successor**

Final contrast:

> **A destructive mythology can make its successful reproduction consume the
> future and eventually itself. A constructive mythology makes disciplined
> transformation create and transmit renewed future possibility.**
